<a id="file-docs-chapters-11-deployment-md"></a>

# 11 — Validate and publish a Windows desktop build

## Purpose and prerequisites

Produce a repeatable deliverable and state its validation limits accurately. Use a Windows workstation with the approved .NET 10 SDK and configured Artifactory feed. Keep work configuration separate from the distributable application.

## Step by step (Windows PowerShell)

1. Run the complete build script:

```powershell
.\scripts\Build.ps1 -Publish
```

It restores in locked mode, builds Release, runs tests, checks generated documentation, and publishes a framework-dependent Windows x64 application to `.artifacts/publish`.

2. Check the target workstation has the .NET 10 **Desktop Runtime**, not only the ASP.NET or base runtime. For an optional self-contained build, use the following; Artifactory must contain the relevant runtime packs, and the build may update runtime-specific restore assets:

```powershell
dotnet publish src/OgcDemo.Wpf -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true --configfile NuGet.local.Config -p:RestoreLockedMode=true -o .artifacts/publish-self-contained
```

3. Copy the entire publish folder to a user-writable test directory. Do not copy only the EXE; native SkiaSharp assets, assemblies, JSON, and samples are required. Trimming and single-file deployment are deliberately not enabled.
4. Place a reviewed `appsettings.local.json` beside the EXE on the destination only when needed. The project excludes private settings from publish output.
5. Run the opt-in Windows integration smoke check with a fresh report name:

```powershell
.\scripts\Test-Desktop.ps1 -Report .artifacts/desktop-smoke-new.json
```

The application creates its real window, exercises its in-process commands and rendered hit testing, checks the unavailable-service path, exports an image, writes a JSON report, and exits. This is not a physical mouse/keyboard walkthrough.
6. Perform the human acceptance pass: pan/wheel zoom, open a local GeoJSON file through the dialog, click shapes/holes, adjust tree controls, resize the window, connect your work services, and verify their real data and authentication. Record server/protocol versions and results without including credentials.

## Production deployment recommendations

Distribute through the organization's approved packaging/signing process. Treat the shipped version and dependency lock as a unit. Keep private configuration and credentials out of packages and source bundles. Choose a writable log directory and define retention explicitly; the demo's daily files do not delete old logs. Test display scaling, graphics drivers, enterprise proxy/TLS behavior, actual layer permissions, and payload sizes on representative workstations.

Windows Integrated Authentication, OAuth/SSO, credential-vault storage, signed installers, updates, and production telemetry are extension work. Do not claim the demo is a finished enterprise product.

## Checkpoint

The published application starts with its local layers, the smoke report records success, and the documentation check reports no stale outputs. Report core/fixture tests, Windows integration checks, manual desktop checks, and actual work-service checks as separate evidence. A successful HTTP response alone is not proof that a map is geographically correct.

## Complete source for this lesson

<a id="file-scripts-build-ps1"></a>

### `scripts/Build.ps1`

Source SHA-256: `15808bed8472125c2059da45313b4f327850935952de5b6565c66d6321bedc6c`

``````powershell
param([string]$NuGetConfig = 'NuGet.local.Config', [switch]$Publish)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-Location (Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent)
if (!(Test-Path $NuGetConfig)) { throw 'Copy NuGet.Config to NuGet.local.Config and replace the Artifactory placeholder.' }
if ((Get-Content $NuGetConfig -Raw).Contains('example.invalid')) { throw 'Configure a real internal Artifactory feed first.' }
function Run-DotNet { & dotnet @args | Out-Host; if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw "dotnet failed with exit code $LASTEXITCODE" } }
Run-DotNet restore OgcDemo.slnx --configfile $NuGetConfig --locked-mode
Run-DotNet build OgcDemo.slnx -c Release --no-restore
Run-DotNet test tests/OgcDemo.Tests -c Release --no-build --no-restore --logger 'trx;LogFileName=tests.trx' --results-directory .artifacts/tests
Run-DotNet test tests/TrafficDemo.Tests -c Release --no-build --no-restore --logger 'trx;LogFileName=traffic-tests.trx' --results-directory .artifacts/tests
Run-DotNet run --project tools/ManualBuilder -c Release --no-build --no-restore -- --check
if ($Publish) {
    Run-DotNet publish src/TrafficDemo.Wpf -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained false --no-restore -o .artifacts/traffic-publish
    Run-DotNet publish src/OgcDemo.Wpf -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained false --no-restore -o .artifacts/publish
}
``````

<a id="file-scripts-test-desktop-ps1"></a>

### `scripts/Test-Desktop.ps1`

Source SHA-256: `80cd9f523d75332017156ecc30e65eedec7cad833203894805f10b48a7aa92ba`

``````powershell
param([string]$Executable = '.artifacts/publish/OgcDemo.Wpf.exe', [string]$Report = '.artifacts/desktop-smoke.json')
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-Location (Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent)
$exe = (Resolve-Path $Executable).Path
$reportPath = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path (Get-Location) $Report))
$parent = Split-Path $reportPath -Parent
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $parent | Out-Null
if (Test-Path $reportPath) { throw 'Choose a new report name; this prevents mistaking an old report for a new test.' }
$p = Start-Process -FilePath $exe -ArgumentList @('--smoke-test', ('"' + $reportPath + '"')) -PassThru
if (!$p.WaitForExit(45000)) { throw 'Smoke test exceeded 45 seconds. Inspect the demo window; no other process was stopped.' }
if (!(Test-Path $reportPath)) { throw 'The app exited without a smoke-test report. Check the desktop runtime and startup configuration.' }
$result = Get-Content $reportPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
if (!$result.success) { throw ('Desktop smoke failed: ' + $result.error) }
$result
``````

<a id="file-src-ogcdemo-wpf-desktopsmoke-cs"></a>

### `src/OgcDemo.Wpf/DesktopSmoke.cs`

Source SHA-256: `ca1a476847d69938645a0c94d48729d5e68e55d6f471545dfe2fae712ab191bb`

``````csharp
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Extensions;
using Mapsui.Layers;
using OgcDemo.Core;

namespace OgcDemo.Wpf;

/// <summary>Opt-in in-process Windows integration check; does not simulate physical mouse/keyboard input.</summary>
internal static class DesktopSmoke
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(MainWindow window, MainViewModel vm, string reportPath)
    {
        var checks = new List<string>();
        void Check(bool condition, string label) { if (!condition) throw new InvalidOperationException(label); checks.Add(label); }
        var success = false; string? error = null;
        try
        {
            await Task.Delay(1500); window.UpdateLayout();
            Check(window.IsVisible, "WPF window is visible");
            Check(vm.Layers.Count == 2, "Bundled graphics and GeoJSON layers loaded");
            Check(vm.Map.Navigator.Viewport.Width > 0, "Map viewport initialized");
            var graphics = (MemoryLayer)vm.Layers.Last().Layer;
            var point = graphics.Features.First();
            var position = vm.Map.Navigator.Viewport.WorldToScreen(point.Extent!.Centroid);
            var hit = window.MapView.GetMapInfo(position, [graphics]);
            Check(hit.Feature != null, "Rendered point hit test finds a feature");
            var line = (Mapsui.Nts.GeometryFeature)graphics.Features.Skip(1).First();
            var coords = line.Geometry!.Coordinates;
            var linePosition = vm.Map.Navigator.Viewport.WorldToScreen(new MPoint((coords[0].X+coords[1].X)/2,(coords[0].Y+coords[1].Y)/2));
            Check(window.MapView.GetMapInfo(linePosition,[graphics]).Feature != null,"Rendered line is pickable between vertices");
            var polygon = (Mapsui.Nts.GeometryFeature)graphics.Features.Last();
            var polygonPosition = vm.Map.Navigator.Viewport.WorldToScreen(polygon.Extent!.Centroid);
            Check(window.MapView.GetMapInfo(polygonPosition,[graphics]).Feature != null,"Rendered polygon is pickable inside its fill");
            await vm.SelectAsync(hit.Feature,position.X,position.Y);
            Check(vm.Details.Contains("Synthetic observation"), "Map selection updates feature details");
            Check(((MemoryLayer)vm.Map.Layers.Last()).Features.Any(), "Selection highlight is populated");
            vm.ClearSelectionCommand.Execute(null);
            Check(!((MemoryLayer)vm.Map.Layers.Last()).Features.Any(), "Selection highlight clears");
            vm.SelectedLayer = vm.Layers[0]; vm.SelectedLayer.Enabled=false;
            Check(!vm.SelectedLayer.Layer.Enabled,"Visibility binds to map layer");
            vm.SelectedLayer.Enabled=true; vm.SelectedLayer.Opacity=0.4;
            Check(Math.Abs(vm.SelectedLayer.Layer.Opacity-0.4)<0.001,"Opacity binds to map layer"); vm.SelectedLayer.Opacity=1;
            var selected=vm.SelectedLayer; vm.MoveDownCommand.Execute(null);
            Check(vm.Layers.Last()==selected && vm.Map.Layers.First()==selected.Layer,"Tree order matches map drawing order");
            vm.MoveUpCommand.Execute(null);
            vm.AddGraphicsCommand.Execute(null); Check(vm.Layers.Count==3,"Dynamic graphics addition works");
            vm.SelectedLayer=vm.Layers[0]; vm.RemoveLayerCommand.Execute(null); Check(vm.Layers.Count==2,"Dynamic removal works");
            vm.HomeCommand.Execute(null); vm.SelectedLayer=vm.Layers.Last();
            await vm.SelectAsync(point,position.X,position.Y);
            await Task.Delay(500); window.UpdateLayout();
            vm.Status="Local vector selection verified. Synthetic training data only.";
            var surface=(FrameworkElement)window.Content; surface.UpdateLayout();
            var bitmap=new RenderTargetBitmap((int)surface.ActualWidth,(int)surface.ActualHeight,96,96,PixelFormats.Pbgra32);
            bitmap.Render(surface); var encoder=new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap));
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(reportPath))!);
            using (var stream=File.Create(Path.ChangeExtension(reportPath,".png"))) encoder.Save(stream);
            vm.SelectedService=new ServiceOptions {Url="https://unavailable.example.invalid/wms",TimeoutSeconds=1};
            await vm.ConnectCommand.ExecuteAsync(null);
            Check(vm.Status.StartsWith("Connection failed:"),"Unavailable service is reported without closing the application");
            Check(window.IsVisible && vm.Layers.Count==2,"Local layers remain usable after service failure");
            success=true;
        }
        catch(Exception e) { error=e.GetType().Name+": "+e.Message; }
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(reportPath))!);
        File.WriteAllText(reportPath,JsonSerializer.Serialize(new {success,checks,error,coverage="In-process WPF integration; not physical-input automation",utc=DateTimeOffset.UtcNow},new JsonSerializerOptions {WriteIndented=true}));
        Application.Current.Shutdown(success?0:1);
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-docs-chapters-12-traffic-viewer-md"></a>

# 12. AIS and ADS-B traffic viewer

The **Traffic Lab** is a third, independent WPF application in this solution. It
receives simulated decoded ship and aircraft reports and measures the cost of
tracking and drawing them in Mapsui. It does not decode radio messages or connect
to real receivers. Every identity and route is synthetic; the authored sea routes
are demonstration waypoints, not navigation data.

The engineering target is 10,000 simultaneous objects. **10,000 is not an enforced
maximum.** Higher nonnegative counts are accepted with a visible warning. The
mailbox and track capacity grow to the configured combined population, so increasing
counts also increases the memory budget. Counts must fit a signed 32-bit total;
this is a representation limit, not a promise that such a population is practical.

## Build and run on Windows

Use the existing .NET 10 SDK/internal NuGet feed instructions from lesson 02.
The Windows Desktop runtime is required; Linux can compile the Windows target but
cannot execute WPF. Keep the repository's pinned package versions and lock files.

```powershell
dotnet restore OgcDemo.slnx --configfile NuGet.local.Config --locked-mode
dotnet build OgcDemo.slnx -c Release --no-restore
dotnet run --project src/TrafficDemo.Wpf -c Release --no-build --no-restore
```

To open an interactive session with 5,000 ships and 5,000 aircraft already running:

```powershell
dotnet run --project src/TrafficDemo.Wpf -c Release --no-build --no-restore -- --ships 5000 --aircraft 5000 --start
```

`--start` begins simulation after the basemap connection attempt and leaves the window
open for interaction. Without it, press Start in the viewer.

To publish all supported full applications and run core checks:

```powershell
./scripts/Build.ps1 -Publish
./scripts/Start-TrafficDemo.ps1
```

The traffic executable is published separately to
`.artifacts/traffic-publish/TrafficDemo.Wpf.exe`. The existing OGC executable keeps
its original output location. Running from source connects to the basemap directly;
the launch helper additionally checks/starts the existing GeoServer deployment.

## GeoServer integration

The default service is `http://127.0.0.1:8602/geoserver`, from the sibling
`small-gis-demos/osm-basemap-demo` project. The server inspected for this work serves
the Eastern Europe bundle, including Greece and the Adriatic coast, with worldwide
Natural Earth overview context. It advertises English Dark Matter, OSM Bright,
Positron, Toner, MapTiler Basic and world layers. The default is
`omt:dark-matter-en`, WMTS 1.0.0, `EPSG:3857`, PNG. BruTile normalizes GeoServer's advertised empty style identifier to `default`; the viewer selects that normalized
style, which this server accepts.
Auxiliary WMTS ResourceURL entries such as TileJSON and FeatureInfo are removed from
the client parser input, leaving only tile templates for the selected image format.
This avoids BruTile 6 treating metadata responses as map images. WMS 1.3.0 is an explicit alternative. `None` selects overlay-only measurement.

The app shares `OgcDemo.Core` transport/capabilities code. Only this viewer opts
into HTTP for loopback endpoints; remote services require HTTPS. Credentials in URLs
and cross-origin capabilities endpoints remain disallowed. No public basemap,
font server, API key or live traffic service is required.

```powershell
# A healthy service is reused, without touching the container engine.
./scripts/Ensure-TrafficBasemap.ps1 -CheckOnly
./scripts/Start-TrafficDemo.ps1

# If the existing deployment is stopped inside WSL, name its distribution.
./scripts/Start-TrafficDemo.ps1 -WslDistribution RHEL10
```

On Linux/WSL, run `bash scripts/ensure-traffic-basemap.sh`. Its default existing
container is `east-europe-bundle_geoserver_1`, using Podman. Override
`TRAFFIC_BASEMAP_CONTAINER` and `TRAFFIC_CONTAINER_ENGINE` for an already prepared
deployment. Windows Podman defaults to `east-europe-bundle`; the PowerShell helper
also accepts an explicit `-ContainerName`. Helpers verify the container's configured
port binding before starting it, including when it is stopped. They do not build or
import a bundle, delete/recreate volumes, pull images, change firewall rules, or start
a Podman machine. If no prepared deployment exists, use that project's documented
bundle launcher first. Starting the app never implicitly provisions infrastructure.

A failed basemap connection leaves simulation usable against the plain background.
The status displays the failure and the **Connect / retry** button retries. A
successful capabilities connection alone is not proof of tile delivery; the benchmark
also records basemap request failures, and the captured map must be inspected.

## Operator workflow

1. Set ship and aircraft counts independently, or stage the 1,000 / 5,000 / 10,000
   mixed presets. Defaults are 500 of each. Preset buttons do not discard a running
   simulation until **Apply configuration and restart** is clicked.
2. Set the random seed, report intervals and simulation speed. Defaults are seed
   42, five seconds for ships, two seconds for aircraft and speed 1. Reports are
   staggered, not delivered as a single population-wide burst.
3. Choose the Adriatic/Greece regional scenario or the dense Ionian Sea scenario.
   The dense home view contains the complete population; there is no clustering.
4. Press **Start**. **Pause** freezes simulation time, route movement and ageing;
   **Resume** continues. **Reset** clears history/selection and leaves the new
   population paused. Applying configuration clears old state and starts afresh.
5. Click a symbol or table row. Details show identity, position, speed, course,
   aircraft altitude, report time/sequence and freshness. **Zoom to selected**
   centers a closer view. **Follow** recenters once per published snapshot. Panning,
   touch movement or mouse-wheel navigation suspends following; check Follow to resume.
6. Use the Filters tab to search identities/names or narrow traffic. The track table
   shows display-eligible tracks across the scenario, not just the current viewport.
7. Use Performance for counters and current-report export. A current-report export
   is not a completed benchmark or a soak test.

Symbols rotate in five-degree display increments using shared styles. Actual report
course remains unquantized in the details. Cyan ship silhouettes and amber aircraft
silhouettes distinguish types. Stale symbols/traces fade. Only the selected object
gets a label by default, avoiding thousands of overlapping text labels.

## Two kinds of filtering

Both panels support ship/aircraft admission, case-insensitive identity/name substring,
speed range in knots, aircraft altitude range in feet, and geographic bounds in
`west,south,east,north` longitude/latitude order. Blank values are unrestricted.
Altitude constraints apply only to aircraft. A west value greater than east denotes
a dateline-crossing geographic filter. Invalid or reversed ranges are rejected.

**Input admission** excludes reports before track updates and purges existing tracks
that no longer qualify. Clearing that filter reconstructs tracks only as new reports
arrive; their discarded history is not restored. The simulator still generates the
full configured population, making generated versus accepted load visible.

**Display filtering** hides symbols, traces and table rows while retaining ingestion,
tracking and bounded history. Clearing it restores the retained data. A selected
track hidden by a filter remains identified in the details panel, while its highlight
and following are suspended. Expired/input-purged selected tracks are similarly marked.
Input filtering and display filtering are separate benchmark operations: changing
one must never be reported as changing the other.

## Configuration and units

`src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/appsettings.json` contains a `Traffic` section. Copy it to
`appsettings.local.json` alongside it for private overrides; the latter is ignored by
Git and excluded from publish output. JSON configuration overlays individual keys.
Editing UI controls affects the current process, not the files on disk.

| Section | Important defaults |
| --- | --- |
| Simulation | Ships 500; Aircraft 500; Seed 42; ShipIntervalSeconds 5; AircraftIntervalSeconds 2; SpeedMultiplier 1; Dense false |
| History | MaximumPoints 150; RetentionSeconds 300; SampleSeconds 2; StaleSeconds 30; ExpireSeconds 120 |
| InputFilter / DisplayFilter | Ships and Aircraft true; Search empty; numeric limits and Bounds unset |
| Basemap | Url loopback port 8602; Layer omt:dark-matter-en; Protocol WMTS; Enabled true |

History intervals, feed timestamps, route progress and ageing use simulation time.
The one-second snapshot schedule, benchmark duration and latency measurements use
wall/monotonic time. At simulation speed 10, five simulated seconds pass in roughly
half a real second. Accelerated scenarios therefore increase report load as well as
movement. A report interval of at least 0.1 simulated seconds is required. History
requires at least two points, positive finite intervals and expiry greater than the
stale interval. Configure stale/expiry above your expected report cadence when using
unusually slow sources. MaximumPoints has a defensive 100,000-per-track bound.

The default map update cadence is once per second, with no millisecond animation or
continuous extrapolation. Aircraft move farther between reports than ships; zoom in
or use simulation speed to make ship movement apparent without falsifying their speed.

## Complete source for this lesson

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-wpf-trafficdemo-wpf-csproj"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/TrafficDemo.Wpf.csproj`

Source SHA-256: `4ee1cdee50e57c0011d62fec538109aad9e85d98d1d577bdb4169c3d7b82e70f`

``````xml
<Project Sdk="Microsoft.NET.Sdk">
  <PropertyGroup>
    <OutputType>WinExe</OutputType><TargetFramework>net10.0-windows10.0.19041.0</TargetFramework>
    <UseWPF>true</UseWPF><EnableWindowsTargeting>true</EnableWindowsTargeting>
    <RuntimeIdentifier>win-x64</RuntimeIdentifier><SelfContained>false</SelfContained><PublishTrimmed>false</PublishTrimmed>
  </PropertyGroup>
  <ItemGroup>
    <ProjectReference Include="../TrafficDemo.Core/TrafficDemo.Core.csproj" />
    <PackageReference Include="Mapsui.Wpf" Version="5.1.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration.Json" Version="10.0.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration.Binder" Version="10.0.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="CommunityToolkit.Mvvm" Version="8.4.0" />
  </ItemGroup>
  <ItemGroup>
    <Content Include="appsettings.json" CopyToOutputDirectory="PreserveNewest" />
    <Content Include="appsettings.local.json" Condition="Exists('appsettings.local.json')" CopyToOutputDirectory="PreserveNewest" CopyToPublishDirectory="Never" />
  </ItemGroup>
</Project>
``````

### `src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/App.xaml.cs`

Source SHA-256: `63772b9c24800ed83f25e18c2e78ef4339c7c9ded17816ab4b2772921b2bfc45`

``````csharp
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;

namespace TrafficDemo.Wpf;
public partial class App : Application
{
    protected override async void OnStartup(StartupEventArgs e)
    {
        base.OnStartup(e);
        try
        {
            var configuration = new ConfigurationBuilder().SetBasePath(AppContext.BaseDirectory).AddJsonFile("appsettings.json")
                .AddJsonFile("appsettings.local.json", optional: true).Build();
            var options = configuration.GetSection("Traffic").Get<ViewerOptions>() ?? new();
            var run = RunOptions.Parse(e.Args); run.Apply(options);
            options.Simulation.Validate(); options.History.Validate(); options.InputFilter.Validate(); options.DisplayFilter.Validate();
            var vm = new MainViewModel(options, Dispatcher); var window = new MainWindow(vm); MainWindow = window; window.Show();
            await vm.ConnectBasemap();
            if (run.Report != null) await DesktopRun.RunAsync(window, run);
            else if (e.Args.Contains("--start")) await vm.StartCommand.ExecuteAsync(null);
        }
        catch (Exception error)
        {
            var reportIndex = Array.IndexOf(e.Args, "--report");
            if (reportIndex >= 0 && reportIndex + 1 < e.Args.Length)
            {
                var path = Path.GetFullPath(e.Args[reportIndex + 1]); Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(path)!);
                // A rejected existing --report must never be overwritten by the error path.
                try
                {
                    using var stream = new FileStream(path, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.None);
                    JsonSerializer.Serialize(stream, new { success = false, error = error.ToString() });
                }
                catch (IOException) when (File.Exists(path)) { }
            }
            else MessageBox.Show(error.Message, "Traffic viewer startup failed", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Error);
            Shutdown(1);
        }
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-wpf-mainwindow-xaml-cs"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/MainWindow.xaml.cs`

Source SHA-256: `b1310abf72a34cff662decf4f6bc5cceb15bff9c45ee5890750e60c8b3721f8e`

``````csharp
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Input;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace TrafficDemo.Wpf;
public partial class MainWindow : Window
{
    public MainViewModel ViewModel { get; }
    private bool _closing;
    private bool _shutdownStarted;
    public MainWindow(MainViewModel viewModel)
    {
        InitializeComponent(); ViewModel = viewModel; DataContext = viewModel;
        MapView.Map = viewModel.Map; MapView.SetMapRenderer(viewModel.Renderer);
        viewModel.Map.Tapped += (_, e) =>
        {
            var info = e.GetMapInfo([viewModel.Symbols]);
            if (info.Feature?["key"] is string key) viewModel.SelectKey(key);
        };
        MapView.PreviewMouseMove += (_, e) => { if (e.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed || e.RightButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed) viewModel.SuspendFollowing(); };
        MapView.PreviewMouseWheel += (_, _) => viewModel.SuspendFollowing();
        MapView.TouchMove += (_, _) => viewModel.SuspendFollowing();
        Closing += async (sender, e) =>
        {
            if (_closing) return; e.Cancel = true;
            if (_shutdownStarted) return;
            _shutdownStarted = true; IsEnabled = false;
            // Yield even when disposal completes synchronously: Close cannot reenter Closing.
            await Task.Yield();
            await viewModel.DisposeAsync(); MapView.Dispose(); _closing = true;
            _ = Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(Close));
        };
    }
    private void ExportClicked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        var dialog = new SaveFileDialog { Filter = "JSON report|*.json", FileName = $"traffic-{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd-HHmmss}.json" };
        if (dialog.ShowDialog(this) == true) BenchmarkReport.Write(dialog.FileName, this, [], 0, "Interactive snapshot; not a completed benchmark or soak.");
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-docs-chapters-13-traffic-data-md"></a>

# 13. Bounded traffic pipeline and map rendering

The design separates decoded observations, current track state, bounded history,
renderable map data and WPF controls. No simulation report creates a WPF element.

```text
SimulatedFeed (background producer)
  → validation + latest-report mailbox (one pending report per identity)
  → TrackStore (one background consumer; admission and order checks)
  → bounded trace rings + current state
  → immutable TrafficSnapshot, once per wall-clock second
  → display filter + zoom simplification + spatial indexes (background)
  → one replaceable pending RenderSnapshot
  → one queued Dispatcher publication
  → indexed Mapsui symbol/trace layers → standard Skia renderer
                                      → completed-render measurements
```

## Contracts and ownership

`ITrackFeed.ReadAsync(CancellationToken)` supplies decoded `TrackReport` values. A
report contains type, identity, name, UTC/simulation timestamp, sequence,
longitude/latitude, knots, course degrees and optional altitude feet. Its key combines
type and identity, so a ship and aircraft with the same textual ID remain distinct.
Real wire-protocol adapters are a future extension behind this interface; the supplied
viewer constructs `SimulatedFeed` and is not a raw AIS/ADS-B decoder.

`ReceivedReport` adds a monotonic ingestion timestamp. The report timestamp determines
ordering/history/age; the monotonic timestamp measures elapsed processing time without
wall-clock adjustment errors. `SimulationClock` advances from monotonic elapsed time
and freezes on pause. Equal seed, configuration, epoch and evaluation time produce
identical simulated positions. The live scheduler naturally has operating-system
jitter; determinism refers to route/attribute evaluation, not exact thread scheduling.

The feed writes only the mailbox. The consumer alone owns `TrackStore` and its trace
rings. Filter configurations are immutable values published atomically. Snapshots
contain detached history arrays; code treats all published data and geometry as
read-only. Geometry/index construction happens on the consumer worker. WPF receives
only a completed snapshot and performs one batched replacement and graphics refresh.
Mapsui owns its own render caches; the app does not dispose native resources while
Mapsui might be drawing them. Shutdown cancels and joins workers before disposing map
resources. Resets replace the engine and use generation checks to discard old work.

## Overload and ordering

The mailbox has capacity equal to the configured population, including populations
above 10,000. A newer report replaces a pending report for the same key. Replacement
increments `Coalesced`; an already full mailbox rejects a new identity and increments
`CapacityRejected`. Invalid reports and non-increasing sequences/backward timestamps
are rejected. The tracker repeats ordering validation against the last accepted state,
rejects future/expired observations, and enforces its configured track capacity.

The simulator never emits an unbounded catch-up burst after a scheduling stall. It
emits the current observation and schedules the next due report. This means the
configured interval is a target cadence, not a promise that every report is generated
under saturation. Generated/accepted counters and process CPU must be considered
alongside latency. Coalescing deliberately loses intermediate observations; it is
appropriate for a current-position viewer, not a lossless recorder.

The UI has one pending snapshot slot and at most one queued publication callback.
A newer snapshot replaces an older unpublished one and increments `ReplacedSnapshots`.
The dispatcher probe also awaits its previous callback, preventing a monitoring
backlog from becoming another load source. A slow renderer can increase latency and
coalescing, but cannot create an unbounded queue of UI updates.

## Trace storage and gaps

Each track has a chronological ring capped by both age and number of points. Default
retention is the last five simulated minutes, at most 150 samples, with a minimum
two-second sample spacing. At the 10,000-object target, the strict upper bound is
**1.5 million retained history points**. Five-second ship reporting normally uses
fewer points than the bound. Memory also includes track metadata, detached snapshot
arrays, symbols, line geometry, spatial indexes, WPF rows and Mapsui/Skia caches; do
not equate the point count with total process memory.

Sequence gaps, sufficiently long report gaps and dateline discontinuities mark a
new trace segment. Unsampled reports still update current position; a pending gap
marker survives until the next retained sample. Rendering never joins these segments
across missing observations. Traces end at the latest retained sample, which can lag
the current symbol by the configured sampling interval.

No report for 30 simulated seconds marks a track stale; 120 seconds expires it and
releases its history. History ages out even when no new reports arrive. Pause freezes
these rules rather than making every paused object stale. Clearing input filters does
not recover discarded samples.

## Rendering choices and costs

Two `IndexedTrafficLayer` instances separate symbols and traces. Their immutable
`FeatureIndex` uses an NTS STRtree, built on the worker and queried with the current
viewport. Symbol queries include a screen-size margin. Trace indexes cover the full
polyline envelope, so a line crossing the viewport remains eligible even when its
current symbol and both endpoints are outside it. Envelope intersection is a broad
phase; Mapsui performs final drawing/clipping.

This replaces the per-query collection scan in the pinned
[Mapsui 5.1.0 MemoryLayer implementation](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Mapsui/Mapsui/5.1.0/Mapsui/Layers/MemoryLayer.cs).
The one-object selection highlight still uses `MemoryLayer`; its collection has at
most one feature, so indexing it would add unnecessary work.

The standard Mapsui renderer draws the actual points and NTS multiline geometries.
This tests Mapsui's map/navigation/rendering path rather than a separate overlay canvas.
No per-track WPF control is placed on the map. The data grid enables row/column
virtualization and recycling; it does not instantiate 10,000 visible rows.

Only two SVG image resources are registered. Symbol styles are shared across type,
staleness and 72 heading buckets; trace styles are also shared. This prevents a
per-report image/style registration leak. Per-track immutable feature caching reuses
unchanged symbols and traces. Cache entries are removed when the track leaves the
eligible set; Mapsui's rendering caches have their own bounded eviction policy.

Douglas–Peucker simplification is applied separately to each displayed trace segment.
The tolerance is 0.75 times a power-of-two bucket of map resolution, in projected
units. The original retained history is never simplified. Resolution bucketing avoids
rebuilding all lines for tiny zoom changes; the next snapshot uses the new zoom bucket.
Selected labels are independent of the traffic style cache. Map rotation is handled
by Mapsui's directional style support.

Per report, current-state/history work is approximately constant. At each snapshot,
filtering and index construction inspect the configured track set; line simplification
cost depends on retained points. Viewport rendering cost depends on the eligible
geometry in view. Spatial indexing helps a zoomed-in view, but cannot eliminate the
cost of drawing every object in a dense 10,000-object view. All eligible symbols remain
present, even when their pixels overlap. The performance harness must expose that cost.

`DataHasChanged` and graphics refresh update the overlays. Routine traffic ticks do
not call `Map.Refresh`, which would request basemap data again. Pan/zoom and explicit
basemap connection changes legitimately trigger tile/image requests. GeoServer remains
a basemap server; moving traffic and history are not uploaded to it.

The retained-point count is a **storage** measurement. It is not the number of vertices
submitted to the renderer: zoom-dependent simplification can reduce a straight trace
to its endpoints. Traces-disabled runs still maintain bounded tracking history, which
isolates much of the drawing/geometry cost from source and history ingestion cost.

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-core-models-cs"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Core/Models.cs`

Source SHA-256: `a6cf14d6341bfe30e2b79ab16ac65a47be984b209a19a665d5ecdd89be35e191`

``````csharp
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace TrafficDemo.Core;

public enum TrackKind { Ship, Aircraft }
public sealed record TrackReport(TrackKind Kind, string Identity, string Name, DateTimeOffset Timestamp,
    long Sequence, double Longitude, double Latitude, double SpeedKnots, double Course, double? AltitudeFeet = null)
{
    public string Key => $"{Kind}:{Identity}";
    public bool IsValid => Enum.IsDefined(Kind) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Identity) && Identity.Length <= 64 &&
        Name is { Length: <= 128 } && Sequence >= 0 && double.IsFinite(Longitude) && Longitude is >= -180 and <= 180 &&
        double.IsFinite(Latitude) && Latitude is >= -85 and <= 85 && double.IsFinite(SpeedKnots) && SpeedKnots is >= 0 and <= 2000 &&
        double.IsFinite(Course) && Course is >= 0 and < 360 &&
        (AltitudeFeet is null || double.IsFinite(AltitudeFeet.Value) && AltitudeFeet is >= -2000 and <= 100000);
}
public sealed record ReceivedReport(TrackReport Report, long ReceivedAt)
{
    public static ReceivedReport Now(TrackReport report) => new(report, Stopwatch.GetTimestamp());
}
public sealed record GeoBounds(double West, double South, double East, double North)
{
    public bool Contains(double lon, double lat) => lat >= South && lat <= North &&
        (West <= East ? lon >= West && lon <= East : lon >= West || lon <= East);
    public bool IsValid => new[] { West, South, East, North }.All(double.IsFinite) && West >= -180 && West <= 180 &&
        East >= -180 && East <= 180 && South >= -85 && North <= 85 && South <= North;
}
public sealed record TrackFilter
{
    public bool Ships { get; init; } = true;
    public bool Aircraft { get; init; } = true;
    public string Search { get; init; } = "";
    public double? MinimumSpeed { get; init; }
    public double? MaximumSpeed { get; init; }
    public double? MinimumAltitude { get; init; }
    public double? MaximumAltitude { get; init; }
    public GeoBounds? Bounds { get; init; }
    public void Validate()
    {
        foreach (var v in new[] { MinimumSpeed, MaximumSpeed, MinimumAltitude, MaximumAltitude })
            if (v.HasValue && !double.IsFinite(v.Value)) throw new ArgumentException("Filter limits must be finite.");
        if (MinimumSpeed > MaximumSpeed || MinimumAltitude > MaximumAltitude || Bounds is { IsValid: false })
            throw new ArgumentException("Filter limits/bounds are invalid.");
    }
    public bool Matches(TrackReport r) => (r.Kind == TrackKind.Ship ? Ships : Aircraft) &&
        (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Search) || r.Identity.Contains(Search.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || r.Name.Contains(Search.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) &&
        (!MinimumSpeed.HasValue || r.SpeedKnots >= MinimumSpeed) && (!MaximumSpeed.HasValue || r.SpeedKnots <= MaximumSpeed) &&
        (r.Kind != TrackKind.Aircraft || ((!MinimumAltitude.HasValue || r.AltitudeFeet >= MinimumAltitude) && (!MaximumAltitude.HasValue || r.AltitudeFeet <= MaximumAltitude))) &&
        (Bounds == null || Bounds.Contains(r.Longitude, r.Latitude));
}
public sealed record SimulationOptions
{
    public int Ships { get; init; } = 500;
    public int Aircraft { get; init; } = 500;
    public int Seed { get; init; } = 42;
    public double ShipIntervalSeconds { get; init; } = 5;
    public double AircraftIntervalSeconds { get; init; } = 2;
    public double SpeedMultiplier { get; init; } = 1;
    public bool Dense { get; init; }
    public int Population => checked(Ships + Aircraft);
    public string Warning => Population > 10000 ? $"Warning: {Population:N0} objects exceeds the 10,000-object validation target; latency and memory may increase." : "";
    public void Validate()
    {
        if (Ships < 0 || Aircraft < 0 || (long)Ships + Aircraft > int.MaxValue) throw new ArgumentException("Counts must be nonnegative and fit in a 32-bit total.");
        if (!double.IsFinite(ShipIntervalSeconds) || !double.IsFinite(AircraftIntervalSeconds) || ShipIntervalSeconds < .1 || AircraftIntervalSeconds < .1)
            throw new ArgumentException("Report intervals must be finite and at least 0.1 seconds.");
        if (!double.IsFinite(SpeedMultiplier) || SpeedMultiplier <= 0 || SpeedMultiplier > 1000) throw new ArgumentException("Simulation speed must be greater than zero and at most 1000.");
    }
}
public sealed record HistoryOptions
{
    public int MaximumPoints { get; init; } = 150;
    public double RetentionSeconds { get; init; } = 300;
    public double SampleSeconds { get; init; } = 2;
    public double StaleSeconds { get; init; } = 30;
    public double ExpireSeconds { get; init; } = 120;
    public void Validate()
    {
        if (MaximumPoints < 2 || MaximumPoints > 100000 || new[] { RetentionSeconds, SampleSeconds, StaleSeconds, ExpireSeconds }.Any(x => !double.IsFinite(x) || x <= 0) || ExpireSeconds <= StaleSeconds)
            throw new ArgumentException("Invalid history limits; expiry must exceed stale time.");
    }
}
public sealed record TracePoint(DateTimeOffset Time, double X, double Y, bool BreakBefore);
public sealed record TrackView(TrackReport Report, long ReceivedAt, bool Stale, TracePoint[] Trace)
{
    public string Key => Report.Key;
    public string Identity => Report.Identity;
    public string Name => Report.Name;
    public TrackKind Kind => Report.Kind;
    public double SpeedKnots => Report.SpeedKnots;
    public double? AltitudeFeet => Report.AltitudeFeet;
}
public sealed record PipelineCounts(long Generated, long Accepted, long Rejected, long Coalesced, long CapacityRejected, int Pending, int Tracked, int Filtered, int HistoryPoints);
public sealed record TrafficSnapshot(long Version, DateTimeOffset SimulationTime, TrackView[] Tracks, PipelineCounts Counts);

/// <summary>Fixed-capacity chronological history; only the tracking owner mutates it.</summary>
public sealed class TraceRing(int capacity)
{
    private readonly TracePoint[] _items = new TracePoint[capacity];
    private int _start;
    public int Count { get; private set; }
    public TracePoint? Last => Count == 0 ? null : _items[(_start + Count - 1) % _items.Length];
    public void Add(TracePoint point)
    {
        if (Count == _items.Length) { _start = (_start + 1) % _items.Length; Count--; }
        _items[(_start + Count++) % _items.Length] = point;
    }
    public void Prune(DateTimeOffset before)
    {
        while (Count > 0 && _items[_start].Time < before) { _start = (_start + 1) % _items.Length; Count--; }
    }
    public TracePoint[] Copy()
    {
        var result = new TracePoint[Count];
        for (var i = 0; i < Count; i++) result[i] = _items[(_start + i) % _items.Length];
        return result;
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-core-simulation-cs"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Core/Simulation.cs`

Source SHA-256: `6e9c1e023430d786b73ab68efb53157f5e64d440a5eee395be904c59d6b4a28f`

``````csharp
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;

namespace TrafficDemo.Core;

public interface ITrackFeed
{
    IAsyncEnumerable<TrackReport> ReadAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken);
}

/// <summary>Simulation time freezes on pause; elapsed time uses a monotonic clock.</summary>
public sealed class SimulationClock(double speed = 1, DateTimeOffset? epoch = null)
{
    private readonly object _gate = new();
    private long _last = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
    private double _seconds;
    private bool _paused = true;
    public DateTimeOffset Epoch { get; } = epoch ?? DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
    public bool Paused { get { lock (_gate) return _paused; } }
    public double Seconds { get { lock (_gate) { Advance(); return _seconds; } } }
    public DateTimeOffset Now => Epoch.AddSeconds(Seconds);
    private void Advance()
    {
        var now = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        if (!_paused) _seconds += Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(_last, now).TotalSeconds * speed;
        _last = now;
    }
    public void SetPaused(bool paused) { lock (_gate) { Advance(); _paused = paused; } }
}

public sealed class SimulatedFeed : ITrackFeed
{
    private sealed record Vessel(TrackKind Kind, string Id, string Name, Route Route, double OffsetKm, double Speed, double Altitude, double Interval, double Phase);
    private readonly Vessel[] _objects;
    private readonly SimulationClock _clock;
    public SimulatedFeed(SimulationOptions options, SimulationClock clock)
    {
        options.Validate(); _clock = clock;
        var random = new Random(options.Seed);
        _objects = new Vessel[options.Population];
        for (var i = 0; i < _objects.Length; i++)
        {
            var ship = i < options.Ships;
            var index = ship ? i : i - options.Ships;
            var routes = ship ? (options.Dense ? Routes.DenseSea : Routes.Sea) : (options.Dense ? Routes.DenseAir : Routes.Air);
            var route = routes[random.Next(routes.Length)];
            var speed = ship ? 8 + random.NextDouble() * 18 : 240 + random.NextDouble() * 240;
            var interval = ship ? options.ShipIntervalSeconds : options.AircraftIntervalSeconds;
            _objects[i] = new(ship ? TrackKind.Ship : TrackKind.Aircraft,
                ship ? (200000000L + index).ToString() : (0xA00000L + index).ToString("X6"),
                $"SIM-{(ship ? "SHIP" : "AIR")}-{index + 1:00000}", route, random.NextDouble() * route.LengthKm,
                speed, ship ? 0 : 12000 + random.Next(27) * 1000, interval, random.NextDouble() * interval);
        }
    }
    // Public deterministic evaluator is also the benchmark/test oracle; it does not alter feed state.
    public TrackReport ReportAt(int index, double seconds, long sequence)
    {
        var v = _objects[index];
        var p = v.Route.Position(v.OffsetKm + seconds * v.Speed * 1.852 / 3600);
        return new(v.Kind, v.Id, v.Name, _clock.Epoch.AddSeconds(seconds), sequence, p.Lon, p.Lat, v.Speed, p.Course,
            v.Kind == TrackKind.Aircraft ? v.Altitude : null);
    }
    public async IAsyncEnumerable<TrackReport> ReadAsync([EnumeratorCancellation] CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var next = _objects.Select(v => v.Phase).ToArray();
        var sequences = new long[_objects.Length];
        using var timer = new PeriodicTimer(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(50));
        while (await timer.WaitForNextTickAsync(cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false))
        {
            if (_clock.Paused) continue;
            var time = _clock.Seconds;
            for (var i = 0; i < next.Length; i++)
            {
                cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();
                if (time < next[i]) continue;
                // Emit the current observation, never an unbounded catch-up burst after suspension.
                next[i] = time + _objects[i].Interval;
                yield return ReportAt(i, time, ++sequences[i]);
            }
        }
    }
}

public sealed class Route(params (double Lon, double Lat)[] points)
{
    private readonly double[] _lengths = Enumerable.Range(0, points.Length - 1).Select(i => Distance(points[i], points[i + 1])).ToArray();
    public double LengthKm => _lengths.Sum();
    public (double Lon, double Lat, double Course) Position(double distanceKm)
    {
        // Traverse back and forth along the authored route: no teleport at its endpoints.
        var length = LengthKm; var distance = distanceKm % (2 * length); var reverse = distance > length;
        if (reverse) distance = 2 * length - distance;
        var segment = 0;
        while (segment < _lengths.Length - 1 && distance > _lengths[segment]) distance -= _lengths[segment++];
        var a = points[segment]; var b = points[segment + 1]; var t = distance / _lengths[segment];
        var course = Math.Atan2((b.Lon - a.Lon) * Math.Cos((a.Lat + b.Lat) * Math.PI / 360), b.Lat - a.Lat) * 180 / Math.PI;
        return (a.Lon + (b.Lon - a.Lon) * t, a.Lat + (b.Lat - a.Lat) * t, (course + (reverse ? 180 : 0) + 360) % 360);
    }
    private static double Distance((double Lon, double Lat) a, (double Lon, double Lat) b)
    {
        var x = (b.Lon - a.Lon) * Math.Cos((a.Lat + b.Lat) * Math.PI / 360); var y = b.Lat - a.Lat;
        return Math.Sqrt(x * x + y * y) * 111.195;
    }
}
public static class Routes
{
    // Offshore demonstration waypoints, not navigational charts or certified routing.
    public static readonly Route[] Sea = [new((13.3,44.3),(15.3,42.5),(18.4,40.5),(19.0,38.2),(20.4,36.2)),
        new((23.7,37.5),(24.0,36.9),(24.5,36.2),(25.5,35.6)), new((19.5,39.4),(19.0,38.3),(19.8,37.0))];
    public static readonly Route[] Air = [new((16.0,45.8),(18.4,43.9),(21.0,41.0),(23.95,37.94)),
        new((12.3,45.5),(16.5,41.2),(20.8,38.0),(24.0,35.4)),new((20.3,44.8),(23.0,40.5),(25.2,37.5))];
    public static readonly Route[] DenseSea = [new((18.8,38.0),(19.3,38.4),(19.8,38.0),(19.3,37.6),(18.8,38.0))];
    public static readonly Route[] DenseAir = [new((18.5,37.5),(20.0,38.5),(18.6,38.4),(20.0,37.5))];
}
``````

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-core-tracking-cs"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Core/Tracking.cs`

Source SHA-256: `807094936632feea77147bbc247848634d3180f14ed26e38186f9517e91856e7`

``````csharp
using OgcDemo.Core;

namespace TrafficDemo.Core;

/// <summary>Bounded latest-value mailbox. Capacity is configured population, not a 10k cap.</summary>
public sealed class LatestReportMailbox(int capacity)
{
    private readonly object _gate = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, ReceivedReport> _pending = new();
    public long Generated { get; private set; }
    public long Coalesced { get; private set; }
    public long Rejected { get; private set; }
    public long CapacityRejected { get; private set; }
    public int Count { get { lock (_gate) return _pending.Count; } }
    public bool Offer(ReceivedReport received)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            Generated++;
            var r = received.Report;
            if (!r.IsValid) { Rejected++; return false; }
            if (_pending.TryGetValue(r.Key, out var old))
            {
                if (r.Sequence <= old.Report.Sequence || r.Timestamp < old.Report.Timestamp) { Rejected++; return false; }
                Coalesced++;
            }
            else if (_pending.Count >= capacity) { CapacityRejected++; return false; }
            _pending[r.Key] = received; return true;
        }
    }
    public ReceivedReport[] Drain()
    {
        lock (_gate) { var items = _pending.Values.ToArray(); _pending.Clear(); return items; }
    }
    public (long Generated, long Coalesced, long Rejected, long CapacityRejected, int Pending) Counters()
    { lock (_gate) return (Generated, Coalesced, Rejected, CapacityRejected, _pending.Count); }
}

public sealed class TrackStore
{
    private sealed class Entry(ReceivedReport latest, int capacity)
    {
        public ReceivedReport Latest = latest;
        public TraceRing History = new(capacity);
        public bool PendingBreak;
    }
    private readonly Dictionary<string, Entry> _tracks = new();
    private readonly HistoryOptions _history;
    private readonly SimulationOptions _simulation;
    private TrackFilter _input = new();
    public long Accepted { get; private set; }
    public long Rejected { get; private set; }
    public int Count => _tracks.Count;
    public int HistoryPointCount => _tracks.Values.Sum(e => e.History.Count);
    public TrackStore(HistoryOptions history, SimulationOptions simulation)
    { history.Validate(); simulation.Validate(); _history = history; _simulation = simulation; }
    public void SetInputFilter(TrackFilter filter)
    {
        filter.Validate(); _input = filter;
        foreach (var key in _tracks.Where(p => !filter.Matches(p.Value.Latest.Report)).Select(p => p.Key).ToArray()) _tracks.Remove(key);
    }
    public bool Accept(ReceivedReport received, DateTimeOffset now)
    {
        var r = received.Report;
        if (!r.IsValid || !_input.Matches(r) || r.Timestamp > now.AddSeconds(1) || (now - r.Timestamp).TotalSeconds >= _history.ExpireSeconds)
        { Rejected++; return false; }
        var exists = _tracks.TryGetValue(r.Key, out var entry);
        if (exists && (r.Sequence <= entry!.Latest.Report.Sequence || r.Timestamp < entry.Latest.Report.Timestamp)) { Rejected++; return false; }
        if (!exists)
        {
            if (_tracks.Count >= _simulation.Population) { Rejected++; return false; }
            entry = new(received, _history.MaximumPoints); _tracks.Add(r.Key, entry);
        }
        else
        {
            var interval = r.Kind == TrackKind.Ship ? _simulation.ShipIntervalSeconds : _simulation.AircraftIntervalSeconds;
            entry!.PendingBreak |= (r.Timestamp - entry.Latest.Report.Timestamp).TotalSeconds > Math.Max(interval * 2.5, _history.SampleSeconds * 2.5) || r.Sequence > entry.Latest.Report.Sequence + 1;
            entry.Latest = received;
        }
        entry!.History.Prune(now.AddSeconds(-_history.RetentionSeconds));
        var last = entry.History.Last;
        if (last == null || (r.Timestamp - last.Time).TotalSeconds >= _history.SampleSeconds)
        {
            var p = Coordinates.Project(r.Longitude, r.Latitude);
            entry.History.Add(new(r.Timestamp, p.X, p.Y, entry.PendingBreak || last != null && Math.Abs(p.X - last.X) > 20037508));
            entry.PendingBreak = false;
        }
        Accepted++; return true;
    }
    public void Age(DateTimeOffset now)
    {
        foreach (var pair in _tracks.ToArray())
        {
            if ((now - pair.Value.Latest.Report.Timestamp).TotalSeconds >= _history.ExpireSeconds) _tracks.Remove(pair.Key);
            else pair.Value.History.Prune(now.AddSeconds(-_history.RetentionSeconds));
        }
    }
    public TrackView[] Snapshot(DateTimeOffset now) => _tracks.Values.Select(e => new TrackView(e.Latest.Report, e.Latest.ReceivedAt,
        (now - e.Latest.Report.Timestamp).TotalSeconds >= _history.StaleSeconds, e.History.Copy())).ToArray();
}

/// <summary>A single background consumer owns the store. The producer only writes the mailbox.</summary>
public sealed class TrafficEngine : IAsyncDisposable
{
    private readonly CancellationTokenSource _stop = new();
    private readonly LatestReportMailbox _mailbox;
    private readonly TrackStore _store;
    private readonly ITrackFeed _feed;
    private readonly Task _producer;
    private readonly Task _consumer;
    private TrackFilter _input;
    private TrackFilter _appliedInput;
    private long _version;
    private Exception? _failure;
    public SimulationClock Clock { get; }
    public Exception? Failure => Volatile.Read(ref _failure);
    public event Action<TrafficSnapshot>? SnapshotReady;
    public TrafficEngine(SimulationOptions simulation, HistoryOptions history, TrackFilter? input = null)
    {
        simulation.Validate(); history.Validate(); _input = input ?? new(); _input.Validate(); _appliedInput = _input;
        Clock = new(simulation.SpeedMultiplier); _mailbox = new(simulation.Population); _store = new(history, simulation); _store.SetInputFilter(_input);
        _feed = new SimulatedFeed(simulation, Clock);
        _producer = Task.Run(Produce); _consumer = Task.Run(Consume);
    }
    public void SetInputFilter(TrackFilter filter) { filter.Validate(); Volatile.Write(ref _input, filter); }
    public void Pause(bool paused) => Clock.SetPaused(paused);
    private async Task Produce()
    {
        try { await foreach (var report in _feed.ReadAsync(_stop.Token)) _mailbox.Offer(ReceivedReport.Now(report)); }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (_stop.IsCancellationRequested) { }
        catch (Exception e) { Volatile.Write(ref _failure, e); _stop.Cancel(); }
    }
    private async Task Consume()
    {
        using var timer = new PeriodicTimer(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100));
        var lastSnapshot = System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        try
        {
            while (await timer.WaitForNextTickAsync(_stop.Token).ConfigureAwait(false))
            {
                var input = Volatile.Read(ref _input);
                if (input != _appliedInput) { _store.SetInputFilter(input); _appliedInput = input; }
                var now = Clock.Now;
                foreach (var item in _mailbox.Drain()) _store.Accept(item, now);
                _store.Age(now);
                if (System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(lastSnapshot).TotalSeconds < 1) continue;
                lastSnapshot = System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
                var counts = _mailbox.Counters();
                var snapshot = new TrafficSnapshot(++_version, now, _store.Snapshot(now),
                    new(counts.Generated, _store.Accepted, counts.Rejected + _store.Rejected, counts.Coalesced, counts.CapacityRejected, counts.Pending, _store.Count, 0, _store.HistoryPointCount));
                SnapshotReady?.Invoke(snapshot);
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (_stop.IsCancellationRequested) { }
        catch (Exception e) { Volatile.Write(ref _failure, e); _stop.Cancel(); }
    }
    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        _stop.Cancel(); await Task.WhenAll(_producer, _consumer).ConfigureAwait(false); SnapshotReady = null; _stop.Dispose();
    }
}
``````

### `src/TrafficDemo.Core/Rendering.cs`

Source SHA-256: `1f92a4c30566ea8c1f71e650e0c0a6aa3d5888923d692c1a1094944561c2f77b`

``````csharp
using System.Diagnostics;
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Layers;
using Mapsui.Nts;
using Mapsui.Styles;
using NetTopologySuite.Geometries;
using NetTopologySuite.Index.Strtree;
using NetTopologySuite.Simplify;
using OgcDemo.Core;

namespace TrafficDemo.Core;

/// <summary>Built once on a worker, then queried read-only by Mapsui and picking.</summary>
public sealed class FeatureIndex
{
    private readonly STRtree<IFeature> _tree = new();
    public IFeature[] Features { get; }
    public MRect? Extent { get; }
    public FeatureIndex(IEnumerable<IFeature> features)
    {
        Features = features.ToArray();
        var extent = new Envelope();
        foreach (var feature in Features)
        {
            if (feature.Extent is not { } box) continue;
            var envelope = new Envelope(box.MinX, box.MaxX, box.MinY, box.MaxY);
            _tree.Insert(envelope, feature); extent.ExpandToInclude(envelope);
        }
        _tree.Build();
        if (!extent.IsNull) Extent = new(extent.MinX, extent.MinY, extent.MaxX, extent.MaxY);
    }
    public IEnumerable<IFeature> Query(MRect? box, double padding = 0) => box == null ? [] :
        _tree.Query(new Envelope(box.MinX - padding, box.MaxX + padding, box.MinY - padding, box.MaxY + padding));
    public static readonly FeatureIndex Empty = new([]);
}
public sealed class IndexedTrafficLayer(string name) : BaseLayer(name)
{
    private FeatureIndex _index = FeatureIndex.Empty;
    public FeatureIndex Index => Volatile.Read(ref _index);
    public override MRect? Extent => Index.Extent;
    public void Publish(FeatureIndex index) => Volatile.Write(ref _index, index);
    public override IEnumerable<IFeature> GetFeatures(MRect? rect, double resolution) => Index.Query(rect, 24 * resolution);
}
public sealed record RenderSnapshot(TrafficSnapshot Source, TrackView[] Visible, FeatureIndex Symbols, FeatureIndex Traces, double PreparationMs, long BuiltAt);

public sealed class TrafficStyles
{
    private readonly ImageStyle[,,] _symbols = new ImageStyle[2, 2, 72];
    private readonly VectorStyle[,] _traces = new VectorStyle[2, 2];
    public TrafficStyles()
    {
        var ship = new Mapsui.Styles.Image { Source = "svg-content://<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' width='24' height='24' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 1 L19 10 L18 22 L6 22 L5 10 Z' fill='#42d6dc' stroke='#102b36' stroke-width='1.5'/><path d='M9 10 H15 V17 H9Z' fill='#d9ffff'/></svg>" };
        var plane = new Mapsui.Styles.Image { Source = "svg-content://<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' width='24' height='24' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M11 1 H13 L14 9 L23 14 V17 L14 14 L14 20 L18 22 V23 L12 22 L6 23 V22 L10 20 L10 14 L1 17 V14 L10 9Z' fill='#ffce65' stroke='#553b10' stroke-width='0.8'/></svg>" };
        for (var kind = 0; kind < 2; kind++) for (var stale = 0; stale < 2; stale++)
        {
            for (var angle = 0; angle < 72; angle++) _symbols[kind, stale, angle] = new()
            { Image = kind == 0 ? ship : plane, SymbolRotation = angle * 5, SymbolScale = .75, RotateWithMap = true, Opacity = stale == 1 ? .35f : 1 };
            _traces[kind, stale] = new() { Line = new Pen(kind == 0 ? new Color(66, 214, 220, stale == 1 ? 55 : 130) : new Color(255, 206, 101, stale == 1 ? 55 : 130), 1.2), Fill = null, Outline = null };
        }
    }
    public IStyle Symbol(TrackView track) => _symbols[(int)track.Kind, track.Stale ? 1 : 0, (int)Math.Round(track.Report.Course / 5) % 72];
    public IStyle Trace(TrackView track) => _traces[(int)track.Kind, track.Stale ? 1 : 0];
}

/// <summary>Worker-owned feature cache. Only immutable published features cross into rendering.</summary>
public sealed class SnapshotBuilder
{
    private sealed record Cached(long Sequence, bool Stale, double Tolerance, DateTimeOffset? First, DateTimeOffset? Last, IFeature Symbol, IFeature? Trace);
    private readonly Dictionary<string, Cached> _cache = new();
    private readonly TrafficStyles _styles = new();
    private readonly GeometryFactory _geometry = new();
    public RenderSnapshot Build(TrafficSnapshot source, TrackFilter display, double resolution, bool traces)
    {
        var start = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        var tolerance = Math.Pow(2, Math.Floor(Math.Log2(Math.Max(1, resolution)))) * .75;
        var visible = source.Tracks.Where(t => display.Matches(t.Report)).ToArray();
        var symbols = new List<IFeature>(visible.Length); var paths = new List<IFeature>(visible.Length);
        var active = new HashSet<string>();
        foreach (var t in visible)
        {
            active.Add(t.Key); _cache.TryGetValue(t.Key, out var cached);
            var first = t.Trace.FirstOrDefault()?.Time; var last = t.Trace.LastOrDefault()?.Time;
            var symbol = cached != null && cached.Sequence == t.Report.Sequence && cached.Stale == t.Stale ? cached.Symbol : CreateSymbol(t);
            var trace = traces ? cached != null && cached.Tolerance == tolerance && cached.First == first && cached.Last == last && cached.Stale == t.Stale
                ? cached.Trace : CreateTrace(t, tolerance) : null;
            _cache[t.Key] = new(t.Report.Sequence, t.Stale, traces ? tolerance : -1, first, last, symbol, trace);
            symbols.Add(symbol); if (trace != null) paths.Add(trace);
        }
        foreach (var key in _cache.Keys.Where(k => !active.Contains(k)).ToArray()) _cache.Remove(key);
        return new(source with { Counts = source.Counts with { Filtered = source.Tracks.Length - visible.Length } }, visible,
            new(symbols), new(paths), Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(start).TotalMilliseconds, Stopwatch.GetTimestamp());
    }
    private IFeature CreateSymbol(TrackView t)
    {
        var p = OgcDemo.Core.Coordinates.Project(t.Report.Longitude, t.Report.Latitude);
        var feature = new PointFeature(p.X, p.Y) { ["key"] = t.Key, ["name"] = t.Name };
        feature.Styles.Add(_styles.Symbol(t)); return feature;
    }
    private IFeature? CreateTrace(TrackView t, double tolerance)
    {
        var segments = new List<LineString>(); var points = new List<Coordinate>();
        void Flush()
        {
            if (points.Count >= 2)
            {
                var line = _geometry.CreateLineString(points.ToArray());
                segments.Add((LineString)DouglasPeuckerSimplifier.Simplify(line, tolerance));
            }
            points.Clear();
        }
        foreach (var p in t.Trace)
        {
            if (p.BreakBefore) Flush();
            points.Add(new(p.X, p.Y));
        }
        Flush(); if (segments.Count == 0) return null;
        var feature = new GeometryFeature(_geometry.CreateMultiLineString(segments.ToArray())) { ["key"] = t.Key };
        feature.Styles.Add(_styles.Trace(t)); return feature;
    }
}
``````

### `src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/FilterEditor.cs`

Source SHA-256: `227359d33de05a05c87f45af217c5ca4b09308a1b9ce6ea448c29fd4cc9fba02`

``````csharp
using System.Globalization;
using TrafficDemo.Core;
namespace TrafficDemo.Wpf;
public sealed class FilterEditor
{
    public bool Ships { get; set; } = true;
    public bool Aircraft { get; set; } = true;
    public string Search { get; set; } = "";
    public string MinimumSpeed { get; set; } = "";
    public string MaximumSpeed { get; set; } = "";
    public string MinimumAltitude { get; set; } = "";
    public string MaximumAltitude { get; set; } = "";
    public string Bounds { get; set; } = "";
    public TrackFilter Build()
    {
        static double? Number(string value) => string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) ? null : double.Parse(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        GeoBounds? box = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Bounds))
        {
            var values = Bounds.Split(',').Select(v => double.Parse(v.Trim(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)).ToArray();
            if (values.Length != 4) throw new ArgumentException("Bounds require west,south,east,north.");
            box = new(values[0], values[1], values[2], values[3]);
        }
        var filter = new TrackFilter { Ships = Ships, Aircraft = Aircraft, Search = Search, MinimumSpeed = Number(MinimumSpeed),
            MaximumSpeed = Number(MaximumSpeed), MinimumAltitude = Number(MinimumAltitude), MaximumAltitude = Number(MaximumAltitude), Bounds = box };
        filter.Validate(); return filter;
    }
    public static FilterEditor From(TrackFilter f) => new() { Ships = f.Ships, Aircraft = f.Aircraft, Search = f.Search,
        MinimumSpeed = f.MinimumSpeed?.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "", MaximumSpeed = f.MaximumSpeed?.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "",
        MinimumAltitude = f.MinimumAltitude?.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "", MaximumAltitude = f.MaximumAltitude?.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "",
        Bounds = f.Bounds == null ? "" : FormattableString.Invariant($"{f.Bounds.West},{f.Bounds.South},{f.Bounds.East},{f.Bounds.North}") };
}
``````

### `src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/MainViewModel.cs`

``````csharp
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Layers;
using Mapsui.Styles;
using OgcDemo.Core;
using TrafficDemo.Core;

namespace TrafficDemo.Wpf;

public partial class MainViewModel : ObservableObject, IAsyncDisposable
{
    private readonly Dispatcher _dispatcher;
    private readonly CancellationTokenSource _stop = new();
    private readonly SemaphoreSlim _resetGate = new(1, 1);
    private readonly SemaphoreSlim _basemapGate = new(1, 1);
    private ConnectedLayer? _basemap;
    private RenderSnapshot? _pending;
    private int _publicationQueued;
    private int _generation;
    private double _resolution = 1000;

    private readonly Task _probe;

    private long _lastCpuAt = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
``````
