``````csharp
    public TrafficMetrics Metrics { get; } = new();
    public Map Map { get; } = new() { CRS = "EPSG:3857", BackColor = new Color(19, 30, 43) };
    public IndexedTrafficLayer Symbols { get; } = new("Traffic symbols") { Style = null };
    public IndexedTrafficLayer Traces { get; } = new("Recent traces") { Style = null };
    public RenderSnapshot? Current { get; private set; }
    public TrafficEngine? Engine => _engine;
    public MeasuredRenderer Renderer { get; }
    public string[] BasemapLayers { get; } = ["omt:dark-matter-en", "omt:osm-bright-en", "omt:positron-en", "omt:toner-en", "omt:maptiler-basic-en", "omt:world-en"];
    public string[] Protocols { get; } = ["WMTS", "WMS", "None"];
    [ObservableProperty] private int _ships;
    [ObservableProperty] private int _aircraft;
    [ObservableProperty] private int _seed;
    [ObservableProperty] private double _shipInterval;
    [ObservableProperty] private double _aircraftInterval;
    [ObservableProperty] private double _simulationSpeed;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _dense;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _showTraces = true;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool _following;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _status = "Ready. Configure population, then Start.";
    [ObservableProperty] private string _basemapStatus = "Basemap not connected.";
    [ObservableProperty] private string _performance = "Waiting for simulation.";
    [ObservableProperty] private string _details = "Select a ship or aircraft.";
    [ObservableProperty] private string _basemapLayer;
    [ObservableProperty] private string _basemapProtocol;
    [ObservableProperty] private TrackView[] _rows = [];
    [ObservableProperty] private TrackView? _selectedTrack;
    [ObservableProperty] private FilterEditor _inputEditor;
    [ObservableProperty] private FilterEditor _displayEditor;
    public string PopulationWarning => (long)Ships + Aircraft > 10000 ? "Above the 10,000-object validation target. Increased latency/memory is possible." : "";
    public MainViewModel(ViewerOptions options, Dispatcher dispatcher)
    {
        Options = options; _dispatcher = dispatcher; _display = options.DisplayFilter;
        var s = options.Simulation; Ships = s.Ships; Aircraft = s.Aircraft; Seed = s.Seed; ShipInterval = s.ShipIntervalSeconds;
        AircraftInterval = s.AircraftIntervalSeconds; SimulationSpeed = s.SpeedMultiplier; Dense = s.Dense;
        _inputEditor = FilterEditor.From(options.InputFilter); _displayEditor = FilterEditor.From(options.DisplayFilter);
        _basemapLayer = options.Basemap.Layer; _basemapProtocol = options.Basemap.Enabled ? options.Basemap.Protocol : "None";
        Map.Layers.Add(Traces); Map.Layers.Add(Symbols); Map.Layers.Add(_selection);
        Map.Navigator.ViewportChanged += (_, _) => Volatile.Write(ref _resolution, Map.Navigator.Viewport.Resolution);
        Renderer = new(Metrics, () => Current, () => _engine?.Clock.Now ?? DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
        Home(); _probe = Task.Run(ProbeDispatcher);
    }
    partial void OnShipsChanged(int value) => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PopulationWarning));
    partial void OnAircraftChanged(int value) => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PopulationWarning));
    partial void OnShowTracesChanged(bool value) => Volatile.Write(ref _drawTraces, value);
    partial void OnFollowingChanged(bool value) { if (value) UpdateSelection(); }
    partial void OnSelectedTrackChanged(TrackView? value)
    {
        if (_updatingRows || value == null) return;
        _selectedKey = value.Key; _lastSelected = value; UpdateSelection();
    }
    public void SelectKey(string key)
    {
        _selectedKey = key; _lastSelected = Current?.Source.Tracks.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Key == key) ?? _lastSelected;
        SelectedTrack = Rows.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Key == key); UpdateSelection();
    }
    public void SuspendFollowing() { if (Following) { Following = false; Status = "Following suspended by map interaction. Use Follow to resume."; } }
    public SimulationOptions ReadSimulation() => new() { Ships = Ships, Aircraft = Aircraft, Seed = Seed, ShipIntervalSeconds = ShipInterval,
        AircraftIntervalSeconds = AircraftInterval, SpeedMultiplier = SimulationSpeed, Dense = Dense };
    [RelayCommand] private async Task Start()
    {
        if (_engine == null) await ResetEngine(true);
        else { _engine.Pause(false); Status = "Running. " + Options.Simulation.Warning; }
    }
    [RelayCommand] private void Pause() { _engine?.Pause(true); Status = "Paused: simulation time and track ageing are frozen."; }
    [RelayCommand] private void Resume() { _engine?.Pause(false); Status = "Running. " + Options.Simulation.Warning; }
    [RelayCommand] private Task Reset() => ResetEngine(false);
    [RelayCommand] private Task ApplyConfiguration() => ResetEngine(true);
    private async Task ResetEngine(bool start)
    {
        await _resetGate.WaitAsync();
        try
        {
            var simulation = ReadSimulation(); simulation.Validate(); Options.History.Validate(); var input = InputEditor.Build();
            Options.Simulation = simulation; Options.InputFilter = input;
            Interlocked.Increment(ref _generation);
            var old = _engine; _engine = null; if (old != null) await old.DisposeAsync();
            Interlocked.Exchange(ref _pending, null); Current = null; Rows = []; _selectedKey = null; _lastSelected = null;
            Symbols.Publish(FeatureIndex.Empty); Traces.Publish(FeatureIndex.Empty); Renderer.Reset(); Following = false; UpdateSelection();
            var builder = new SnapshotBuilder(); var generation = _generation;
            // Potentially large simulator population is allocated on a worker, never on the UI thread.
            _engine = await Task.Run(() => new TrafficEngine(simulation, Options.History, input));
            _engine.SnapshotReady += source =>
            {
                var prepared = builder.Build(source, Volatile.Read(ref _display), Volatile.Read(ref _resolution), Volatile.Read(ref _drawTraces));
                if (generation != Volatile.Read(ref _generation) || _stop.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                Metrics.PreparationMs.Add(prepared.PreparationMs);
                if (Interlocked.Exchange(ref _pending, prepared) != null) Interlocked.Increment(ref Metrics.ReplacedSnapshots);
                QueuePublication();
            };
            _engine.Pause(!start); Home(); Map.RefreshGraphics();
            Status = (start ? "Running. " : "Reset and paused. ") + simulation.Warning;
        }
        catch (Exception e) when (e is ArgumentException or FormatException or OverflowException) { Status = e.Message; }
        finally { _resetGate.Release(); }
    }
    private void QueuePublication()
    {
        if (_stop.IsCancellationRequested || Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref _publicationQueued, 1, 0) != 0) return;
        _dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Background, new Action(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                var prepared = Interlocked.Exchange(ref _pending, null);
                if (prepared == null || _stop.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                var start = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp(); Current = prepared;
                Symbols.Publish(prepared.Symbols); Traces.Publish(prepared.Traces);
                _updatingRows = true;
                try { Rows = prepared.Visible; SelectedTrack = Rows.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Key == _selectedKey); }
                finally { _updatingRows = false; }
                UpdateSelection();
                // Overlay-only invalidation. No Map.Refresh(), which would refetch the basemap.
                Symbols.DataHasChanged(); Traces.DataHasChanged(); Map.RefreshGraphics();
                Metrics.PublicationMs.Add(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(start).TotalMilliseconds);
                UpdatePerformance();
            }
            finally { Interlocked.Exchange(ref _publicationQueued, 0); if (Volatile.Read(ref _pending) != null) QueuePublication(); }
        }));
    }
    private void UpdateSelection()
    {
        var selected = Current?.Source.Tracks.FirstOrDefault(t => t.Key == _selectedKey);
        if (selected != null) _lastSelected = selected;
        if (_lastSelected == null) { Details = "Select a ship or aircraft."; _selection.Features = []; _selection.DataHasChanged(); return; }
        var t = _lastSelected; var shown = selected != null && Volatile.Read(ref _display).Matches(t.Report);
        if (!shown) Following = false;
        Details = $"{t.Name}\n{t.Kind} · {t.Identity}\n{t.Report.Latitude:F5}, {t.Report.Longitude:F5}\n{t.SpeedKnots:F1} kn · course {t.Report.Course:F0}°" +
            (t.AltitudeFeet.HasValue ? $" · {t.AltitudeFeet:N0} ft" : "") + $"\nReport {t.Report.Timestamp:HH:mm:ss} UTC · sequence {t.Report.Sequence}\n" +
            (selected == null ? "Not currently tracked (input-filtered, expired or reset)." : !shown ? "Hidden by display filter; following suspended." : t.Stale ? "Stale report" : "Current report");
        if (shown)
        {
            var p = Coordinates.Project(t.Report.Longitude, t.Report.Latitude);
            var feature = new PointFeature(p.X, p.Y);
            feature.Styles.Add(new SymbolStyle { SymbolScale = 1.15, Fill = null, Outline = new Pen(Color.White, 2) });
            feature.Styles.Add(new LabelStyle { Text = t.Name, ForeColor = Color.White, BackColor = new Brush(new Color(20, 35, 50, 220)), Offset = new Offset(0, 24) });
            _selection.Features = [feature];
            if (Following) Map.Navigator.CenterOn(new MPoint(p.X, p.Y), 0);
        }
        else _selection.Features = [];
        _selection.DataHasChanged();
    }
    [RelayCommand] private void Focus()
    {
        if (_lastSelected is not { } t || !Volatile.Read(ref _display).Matches(t.Report)) return;
        var p = Coordinates.Project(t.Report.Longitude, t.Report.Latitude); Map.Navigator.CenterOnAndZoomTo(new MPoint(p.X, p.Y), 200, 0);
    }
    [RelayCommand] private void Home()
    {
        var a = Coordinates.Project(Dense ? 18.3 : 11.5, Dense ? 37.2 : 34.5); var b = Coordinates.Project(Dense ? 20.3 : 26.5, Dense ? 38.8 : 46.5);
        Map.Navigator.ZoomToBox(new MRect(a.X, a.Y, b.X, b.Y), duration: 0);
    }
    [RelayCommand] private void Preset(string value)
    {
        if (!int.TryParse(value, out var count)) return; Ships = count / 2; Aircraft = count - Ships;
        Status = "Preset staged. Apply configuration to reset and run.";
    }
    [RelayCommand] private void ApplyInputFilter()
    {
        try { var filter = InputEditor.Build(); Options.InputFilter = filter; _engine?.SetInputFilter(filter); Status = "Input filter applied; excluded tracks are purged on the next processing cycle."; }
        catch (Exception e) when (e is ArgumentException or FormatException or OverflowException) { Status = "Input filter: " + e.Message; }
    }
    [RelayCommand] private void ApplyDisplayFilter()
    {
        try { var filter = DisplayEditor.Build(); Options.DisplayFilter = filter; Volatile.Write(ref _display, filter); UpdateSelection(); Status = "Display filter applied; tracking and retained history continue."; }
        catch (Exception e) when (e is ArgumentException or FormatException or OverflowException) { Status = "Display filter: " + e.Message; }
    }
    [RelayCommand] private void ClearDisplayFilter() { DisplayEditor = new(); ApplyDisplayFilter(); }
    [RelayCommand] private void ClearInputFilter() { InputEditor = new(); ApplyInputFilter(); }
    [RelayCommand] public async Task ConnectBasemap()
    {
        await _basemapGate.WaitAsync();
        try
        {
            if (_basemap != null) { Map.Layers.Remove(_basemap.Layer); _basemap.Dispose(); _basemap = null; }
            Options.Basemap.Layer = BasemapLayer; Options.Basemap.Protocol = BasemapProtocol; Options.Basemap.Enabled = BasemapProtocol != "None";
            if (BasemapProtocol == "None") { BasemapStatus = "Overlay-only mode."; Map.RefreshGraphics(); return; }
            var baseUrl = new Uri(Options.Basemap.Url.TrimEnd('/') + "/");
            if (baseUrl.UserInfo.Length != 0 || !(baseUrl.Scheme == "https" || baseUrl.Scheme == "http" && baseUrl.IsLoopback)) throw new ArgumentException("Basemap requires HTTPS or loopback HTTP, without URL credentials.");
            var o = new ServiceOptions { Name = "OSM basemap", Kind = BasemapProtocol, Version = BasemapProtocol == "WMTS" ? "1.0.0" : "1.3.0",
                Url = new Uri(baseUrl, BasemapProtocol == "WMTS" ? "gwc/service/wmts" : "wms").AbsoluteUri, Layer = BasemapLayer, MatrixSet = "EPSG:3857", Style = BasemapProtocol == "WMTS" ? "default" : "", TimeoutSeconds = 15 };
            BasemapStatus = "Connecting to local GeoServer…";
            var transport = new OgcTransport(o, new BasemapHandler(Metrics), allowLoopbackHttp: true);
            try { _basemap = await OgcLayers.ConnectAsync(o, transport, _stop.Token); }
            catch { transport.Dispose(); throw; }
            _basemap.Layer.DataChanged += (_, e) => { if (e?.Error != null) Interlocked.Increment(ref Metrics.BasemapFailures); };
            Map.Layers.Insert(0, _basemap.Layer); Map.Refresh(); BasemapStatus = $"{BasemapProtocol} · {BasemapLayer}";
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (_stop.IsCancellationRequested) { }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            Interlocked.Increment(ref Metrics.BasemapFailures); BasemapStatus = $"Basemap unavailable: {(e is DemoException or ArgumentException ? e.Message : e.GetType().Name)} Simulation remains usable. Retry Connect.";
        }
        finally { _basemapGate.Release(); }
    }
    private async Task ProbeDispatcher()
    {
        using var timer = new PeriodicTimer(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(250));
        try
        {
            while (await timer.WaitForNextTickAsync(_stop.Token))
            {
                var start = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
                await _dispatcher.InvokeAsync(() =>
                {
                    Metrics.DispatcherMs.Add(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(start).TotalMilliseconds);
                    if (_engine?.Failure is { } failure) Status = $"Simulation stopped: {failure.GetType().Name}: {failure.Message}";
                }, DispatcherPriority.Background, _stop.Token);
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (_stop.IsCancellationRequested) { }
    }
    private void UpdatePerformance()
    {
        if (Current == null) return; var c = Current.Source.Counts; _process.Refresh();
        var now = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp(); var cpu = _process.TotalProcessorTime;
        var percent = (cpu - _lastCpu).TotalSeconds / Math.Max(.001, Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(_lastCpuAt, now).TotalSeconds) / Environment.ProcessorCount * 100;
        _lastCpu = cpu; _lastCpuAt = now;
        var latency = Metrics.IngestionToRenderMs.Summary(); var render = Metrics.RenderMs.Summary();
        Performance = $"Generated {c.Generated:N0} · accepted {c.Accepted:N0}\nRejected {c.Rejected:N0} · coalesced {c.Coalesced:N0} · capacity rejected {c.CapacityRejected:N0}\n" +
            $"Tracked {c.Tracked:N0} · filtered {c.Filtered:N0} · eligible {Current.Visible.Length:N0}\nDrawn symbols {Renderer.RenderedSymbols:N0} · traces {Renderer.RenderedTraces:N0}\n" +
            $"History {c.HistoryPoints:N0} pts · mailbox {c.Pending:N0} · pending snapshot {(_pending == null ? 0 : 1)}\n" +
            $"Ingest→render p95 {latency.P95:F0} / p99 {latency.P99:F0} ms\nReport age p95 {Metrics.ReportAgeSeconds.Summary().P95:F1} s\n" +
            $"Render p95 {render.P95:F1} ms · UI delay p95 {Metrics.DispatcherMs.Summary().P95:F1} ms\n" +
            $"Prepare {Current.PreparationMs:F1} ms · publish p95 {Metrics.PublicationMs.Summary().P95:F1} ms\n" +
            $"CPU {percent:F1}% · private {_process.PrivateMemorySize64 / 1048576:N0} MiB · managed {GC.GetTotalMemory(false) / 1048576:N0} MiB\n" +
            $"Allocated {GC.GetTotalAllocatedBytes() / 1048576:N0} MiB · GC {GC.CollectionCount(0)}/{GC.CollectionCount(1)}/{GC.CollectionCount(2)}\n" +
            $"Basemap images {Metrics.BasemapImageResponses} · failures {Metrics.BasemapFailures} · replaced snapshots {Metrics.ReplacedSnapshots}";
        if (_engine?.Failure is { } error) Status = $"Simulation stopped: {error.GetType().Name}: {error.Message}";
    }
    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        _stop.Cancel(); Interlocked.Increment(ref _generation);
        await _resetGate.WaitAsync();
        try { if (_engine != null) await _engine.DisposeAsync(); }
        finally { _resetGate.Release(); }
        await _probe; await _basemapGate.WaitAsync();
        try { if (_basemap != null) { Map.Layers.Remove(_basemap.Layer); _basemap.Dispose(); } }
        finally { _basemapGate.Release(); }
        Map.Dispose(); _process.Dispose(); _stop.Dispose();
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-docs-chapters-14-traffic-performance-md"></a>

# 14. Measuring traffic performance

Run Release builds on Windows. A successful build or core test cannot establish WPF
rendering performance. Measurements belong to the recorded hardware, viewport/DPI,
population, timing, history and basemap/cache configuration.

## Repeatable commands

```powershell
./scripts/Test-TrafficDesktop.ps1 -Report .artifacts/traffic/smoke-new.json
./scripts/Benchmark-Traffic.ps1 -Seconds 60
./scripts/Benchmark-Traffic.ps1 -Seconds 60 -FullMatrix -Soak
```

Each runner refuses an existing report/output location. The default matrix is 18
sequential cases: 1,000, 5,000 and 10,000 objects; all ships, all aircraft and mixed;
with GeoServer and overlay-only. It uses a dense viewport and traces. `-FullMatrix`
adds dispersed views and trace-disabled comparisons for all populations/types/basemap
modes. The scripted interaction cycle selects/follows a track, returns home, applies
and clears a display filter, and pans. Fixed-view runs are available directly through
the executable. `-Soak` adds a 30-minute, 10,000-object mixed dense run with traces and
GeoServer. Do not run multiple benchmark apps simultaneously for baseline evidence.
Keep the benchmark window visible, use a consistent window size/DPI, and avoid other
heavy workloads during a comparison. Minimizing or manually navigating the window
changes the rendering workload and should be recorded as a separate experiment.

```powershell
.artifacts/traffic-publish/TrafficDemo.Wpf.exe --report C:\Temp\traffic-10k.json --seconds 1800 --ships 5000 --aircraft 5000 --dense
.artifacts/traffic-publish/TrafficDemo.Wpf.exe --report C:\Temp\traffic-overlay.json --seconds 60 --ships 5000 --aircraft 5000 --dense --no-basemap
```

Additional arguments are `--no-traces`, `--exercise`, and `--smoke`. Native runs export
JSON, a CSV process time series and a final PNG. The interactive Performance tab can
export current JSON/CSV without completing a timed test. JSON records the configuration,
viewport, DPI, runtime, processor description, Mapsui version and cache conditions.
The server cache is deliberately not flushed; each new app starts with a new client
cache. This is a repeatable workload, not a claim of a cold server-cache comparison.

## What the measurements mean

| Measurement | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| Generated | Reports actually offered by the simulator; cadence may fall under saturation |
| Accepted / rejected | Tracker admissions and validation/filter/order rejections |
| Coalesced | Pending reports superseded by a newer report for the same identity |
| CapacityRejected | New identities rejected by a full mailbox |
| Tracked / filtered / eligible | Current state, excluded display state, and eligible tracks |
| Drawn symbols / traces | Spatial-query candidates submitted to Mapsui for the viewport; overlapping symbols still count individually |
| Ingestion-to-render ms | Monotonic receive timestamp to first completed on-screen Mapsui render for a new visible report |
| Report age seconds | Simulation-time age of reports submitted in a completed frame |
| Preparation / publication ms | Worker geometry/index construction and batched UI publication respectively |
| Render ms | Time inside the actual Mapsui renderer's `Render` call |
| Dispatcher ms | Delay until a background-priority UI callback executes |
| Memory / allocation / GC | Private, working-set and managed bytes; cumulative allocated bytes and GC counts |
| CPU | Process CPU time; dashboard normalizes usage over logical processors |

Preparation timing starts after the tracking-state/history snapshot copy; ingestion-to-render
latency still includes that earlier work.

`MeasuredRenderer` forwards rendering, drawable creation, picking and bitmap export
to the pinned standard renderer. Timing is recorded **after** drawing returns; merely
queuing a refresh is not a completed-frame measurement. It does not measure physical
monitor scan-out. Bitmap export is not counted as an on-screen frame.

Ingestion latency is recorded once per newly rendered track sequence. Reports replaced
before rendering are not latency samples: coalescing and snapshot-replacement counters
must therefore accompany the percentile. Offscreen tracks are not drawn and do not
produce rendering-latency samples until in view. A newly visible report can already be
several seconds old. Report-age measurements include that staleness independently.

Metric buffers retain at most 20,000 recent samples per metric for exact dashboard
percentiles. Separate fixed histograms accumulate the whole run without retaining
individual samples. Benchmark acceptance uses `LifetimeP95UpperBound` and
`LifetimeP99UpperBound`: 1 ms buckets for timings, 0.01 s buckets for report age.
The overflow bucket is above 60 seconds for timings (600 seconds for report age);
a percentile falling there is null and fails the corresponding target. These are
conservative bucket upper bounds. Long-run stability must also be assessed using
the CSV, counters and scenario coverage, rather than a good final-window percentile. Process samples are bounded to
approximately 720 per timed run. Dashboard CPU and allocations include the application,
not just the renderer, and the renderer wrapper itself adds measurement overhead.

## Acceptance targets

For 10,000 objects with normal report intervals and bounded traces:

- p95 ingestion-to-render at most 3 seconds; p99 at most 5 seconds.
- p95 dispatcher response at most 250 ms during the interaction workload.
- No queue, track or retained-history count above its configured bound.
- The full configured population must be tracked; dense-view symbols must be checked
  against that population. Dispersed/zoomed views legitimately draw fewer symbols.
- A 30-minute soak with stable post-warm-up memory. The report calculates a private-byte
  linear trend after minute 10; at most 1 MiB/min is the explicit diagnostic threshold.
  Inspect the time series as well, because a threshold alone cannot prove absence of leaks.

`success` means the run completed its functional checks with a population and native
frames. `acceptance` reports latency, dispatcher, bounds and soak separately. A short
run has `soakEvaluated: false`, not a passing soak. Basemap failures are reported
separately and fail baseline acceptance; a working overlay cannot establish GeoServer success. A failure is not
fixed by silently disabling traces, clustering objects, reducing counts or changing
report intervals. Such changes are distinct, clearly labelled comparison cases.

## Test coverage and troubleshooting

Core tests cover deterministic simulation, speed/direction changes, pause/cancellation,
report validation and ordering, mailbox replacement/capacity, time/count history limits,
sequence/time/dateline gaps, stale/expiry transitions, input versus display semantics,
spatial queries for crossing traces, immutable render snapshots, trace simplification,
metric bounds, engine shutdown and intact 1k/5k/10k/12k populations.

The Windows smoke run verifies native rendering, symbol hit testing, trace publication,
selection, following suspension, pause, display filtering without state loss, input
purging, reset/start, and recoverable GeoServer failure. It is in-process integration,
not physical mouse/keyboard automation. Inspect its PNG for visual correctness and
record separately whether a human or desktop automation exercised physical input.

- **No window/report:** verify `dotnet --list-runtimes` contains both .NET 10 and
  Microsoft.WindowsDesktop.App. An empty leftover SDK directory is not an installation.
- **Plain background:** check both WMS capabilities and WMTS tile delivery from the
  Windows host. A WSL-only curl result does not establish Windows access.
- **No selected track after filtering:** the details explain whether it is hidden,
  purged or expired; clear the relevant filter and wait for new reports where necessary.
- **Traces appear short:** ships move slowly, retained history starts empty, and the
  trace ends at the latest sampled observation. Zoom in, wait, or deliberately change
  simulation speed. Higher speed also increases input load.
- **High memory:** inspect population ×MaximumPoints and GeoServer separately; private
  bytes include native drawing/cache allocations as well as managed objects.
- **High latency or coalescing:** compare preparation, publication and rendering costs;
  compare overlay-only versus GeoServer runs and inspect GC/CPU. Keep all configuration
  differences in the report rather than attributing every delay to Mapsui.

See [validation](#file-docs-validation-md) for the actual checked-out implementation's results.

Timing/GC counters belong to the application session. Interactive Reset/Apply clears
simulation state and its admission counters, but retains profiler history. Start a
fresh process for configuration comparisons; the benchmark runner does this for every
case. Smoke tests intentionally pause/reset/filter and are functional checks, so their
latency acceptance fields are not representative steady-load performance results.

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-core-metrics-cs"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Core/Metrics.cs`

Source SHA-256: `1f9667770f45cf14ff1182a000de9b10f4b633f1461e397285fc5504fe56146e`

``````csharp
namespace TrafficDemo.Core;

/// <summary>Bounded recent sample window. Percentiles describe this window, not all past samples.</summary>
public sealed class MetricWindow(int capacity = 20000, double bucketWidth = 1)
{
    private readonly object _gate = new();
    private readonly double[] _values = new double[capacity];
    private int _next, _count;
    private readonly long[] _histogram = new long[60002];
    public long TotalSamples { get; private set; }
    public void Add(double value)
    {
        if (!double.IsFinite(value)) return;
        lock (_gate) { _values[_next] = value; _next = (_next + 1) % capacity; _count = Math.Min(_count + 1, capacity); TotalSamples++; _histogram[Math.Clamp((int)Math.Min(60001, Math.Ceiling(Math.Max(0, value) / bucketWidth)), 0, 60001)]++; }
    }
    public MetricSummary Summary()
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (_count == 0) return new(0, TotalSamples, null, null, null, null, null, null);
            var sorted = _values.Take(_count).Order().ToArray();
            double P(double p) => sorted[Math.Clamp((int)Math.Ceiling(p * sorted.Length) - 1, 0, sorted.Length - 1)];
            double? Lifetime(double percentile)
            {
                var rank = (long)Math.Ceiling(TotalSamples * percentile); long cumulative = 0;
                for (var i = 0; i < _histogram.Length; i++)
                {
                    cumulative += _histogram[i];
                    if (cumulative >= rank) return i == 60001 ? null : i * bucketWidth;
                }
                return null;
            }
            return new(_count, TotalSamples, sorted.Average(), P(.95), P(.99), sorted[^1], Lifetime(.95), Lifetime(.99));
        }
    }
}
public sealed record MetricSummary(int WindowSamples, long TotalSamples, double? Mean, double? P95, double? P99, double? Max, double? LifetimeP95UpperBound, double? LifetimeP99UpperBound);
public sealed class TrafficMetrics
{
    public MetricWindow IngestionToRenderMs { get; } = new();
    public MetricWindow ReportAgeSeconds { get; } = new(bucketWidth: .01);
    public MetricWindow RenderMs { get; } = new();
    public MetricWindow PreparationMs { get; } = new();
    public MetricWindow PublicationMs { get; } = new();
    public MetricWindow DispatcherMs { get; } = new();
    public long BasemapFailures;
    public long BasemapImageResponses;
    public long ReplacedSnapshots;
    public object Summary() => new { ingestionToRenderMs = IngestionToRenderMs.Summary(), reportAgeSeconds = ReportAgeSeconds.Summary(),
        renderMs = RenderMs.Summary(), preparationMs = PreparationMs.Summary(), publicationMs = PublicationMs.Summary(), dispatcherMs = DispatcherMs.Summary(),
        basemapImageResponses = Interlocked.Read(ref BasemapImageResponses), basemapFailures = Interlocked.Read(ref BasemapFailures), replacedSnapshots = Interlocked.Read(ref ReplacedSnapshots) };
}
``````

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-wpf-basemaphandler-cs"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/BasemapHandler.cs`

Source SHA-256: `7b309b12315b8f06d60ad6ef751c309b4040a109e1c39fd43e3fc4fe793b8714`

``````csharp
using System.Net.Http;
using TrafficDemo.Core;
namespace TrafficDemo.Wpf;
/// <summary>Count actual image HTTP responses independently of capabilities connection status.</summary>
internal sealed class BasemapHandler(TrafficMetrics metrics) : DelegatingHandler(new HttpClientHandler { AllowAutoRedirect = false })
{
    protected override async Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendAsync(HttpRequestMessage request, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var response = await base.SendAsync(request, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
        if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode && response.Content.Headers.ContentType?.MediaType?.StartsWith("image/", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true)
            Interlocked.Increment(ref metrics.BasemapImageResponses);
        return response;
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-wpf-measuredrenderer-cs"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/MeasuredRenderer.cs`

Source SHA-256: `6450b6b591740a6371d3b95ef2c944539ba21870b9f93fb7ceeed7aedf7d01e5`

``````csharp
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Extensions;
using Mapsui.Layers;
using Mapsui.Manipulations;
using Mapsui.Rendering;
using Mapsui.Styles;
using Mapsui.Widgets;
using TrafficDemo.Core;

namespace TrafficDemo.Wpf;

/// <summary>Delegates all drawing/picking to Mapsui and measures completed on-screen render calls.</summary>
public sealed class MeasuredRenderer(TrafficMetrics metrics, Func<RenderSnapshot?> snapshot, Func<DateTimeOffset> simulationNow) : IMapRenderer
{
    private readonly IMapRenderer _inner = new Mapsui.Rendering.Skia.MapRenderer();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, long> _renderedSequence = new();
    private RenderSnapshot? _last;
    public int RenderedSymbols { get; private set; }
    public int RenderedTraces { get; private set; }
    public long Frames { get; private set; }
    public void Reset() { _renderedSequence.Clear(); _last = null; RenderedSymbols = 0; RenderedTraces = 0; }
    public void Render(object target, Viewport viewport, IEnumerable<ILayer> layers, IEnumerable<IWidget> widgets,
        RenderService service, Color? background = null, MRect? dirtyRegion = null, CoordinateSpace coordinateSpace = CoordinateSpace.World)
    {
        var current = snapshot(); var start = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        _inner.Render(target, viewport, layers, widgets, service, background, dirtyRegion, coordinateSpace);
        var completed = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp(); metrics.RenderMs.Add(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(start, completed).TotalMilliseconds); Frames++;
        if (current == null) return;
        var extent = viewport.ToExtent();
        var features = current.Symbols.Query(extent, 24 * viewport.Resolution).ToArray();
        RenderedSymbols = features.Length; RenderedTraces = current.Traces.Query(extent).Count();
        if (ReferenceEquals(current, _last)) return;
        _last = current;
        var visible = current.Visible.ToDictionary(t => t.Key);
        var now = simulationNow();
        foreach (var feature in features)
        {
            var key = (string)feature["key"]!; var track = visible[key];
            metrics.ReportAgeSeconds.Add(Math.Max(0, (now - track.Report.Timestamp).TotalSeconds));
            if (!_renderedSequence.TryGetValue(key, out var sequence) || sequence != track.Report.Sequence)
            {
                metrics.IngestionToRenderMs.Add(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(track.ReceivedAt, completed).TotalMilliseconds);
                _renderedSequence[key] = track.Report.Sequence;
            }
        }
        foreach (var key in _renderedSequence.Keys.Where(k => !visible.ContainsKey(k)).ToArray()) _renderedSequence.Remove(key);
    }
    public void UpdateDrawables(Viewport viewport, ILayer layer, RenderService service) => _inner.UpdateDrawables(viewport, layer, service);
    public IDrawable? CreateDrawableForFeature(Viewport viewport, ILayer layer, IFeature feature, IStyle style, RenderService service) => _inner.CreateDrawableForFeature(viewport, layer, feature, style, service);
    public MemoryStream RenderToBitmapStream(Viewport viewport, IEnumerable<ILayer> layers, RenderService service, Color? background = null, float pixelDensity = 1,
        IEnumerable<IWidget>? widgets = null, RenderFormat renderFormat = RenderFormat.Png, int quality = 100) => _inner.RenderToBitmapStream(viewport, layers, service, background, pixelDensity, widgets, renderFormat, quality);
    public bool TryGetWidgetRenderer(Type type, out IWidgetRenderer? renderer) => _inner.TryGetWidgetRenderer(type, out renderer);
    public bool TryGetStyleRenderer(Type type, out IStyleRenderer? renderer) => _inner.TryGetStyleRenderer(type, out renderer);
    public MapInfo GetMapInfo(ScreenPosition position, Viewport viewport, IEnumerable<ILayer> layers, RenderService service, int margin = 0) => _inner.GetMapInfo(position, viewport, layers, service, margin);
}
``````

<a id="file-src-trafficdemo-wpf-desktoprun-cs"></a>

### `src/TrafficDemo.Wpf/DesktopRun.cs`

Source SHA-256: `18184045b28a86010c56339cc44a2249a5a9a5e3846df9ecd0272320c2152d55`

``````csharp
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Extensions;
using TrafficDemo.Core;

namespace TrafficDemo.Wpf;
public sealed record RunOptions(string? Report, int Seconds, int? Ships, int? Aircraft, bool Dense, bool NoBasemap, bool NoTraces, bool Smoke, bool Exercise)
{
    public static RunOptions Parse(string[] args)
    {
        string? Value(string key) { var i = Array.IndexOf(args, key); return i < 0 ? null : i + 1 < args.Length ? args[i + 1] : throw new ArgumentException("Missing " + key + " value"); }
        int? Number(string key) => Value(key) is { } v ? int.Parse(v, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) : null;
        var result = new RunOptions(Value("--report"), Number("--seconds") ?? 60, Number("--ships"), Number("--aircraft"), args.Contains("--dense"),
            args.Contains("--no-basemap"), args.Contains("--no-traces"), args.Contains("--smoke"), args.Contains("--exercise"));
        if (result.Seconds < 5 || result.Seconds > 86400) throw new ArgumentException("Benchmark duration must be 5–86400 seconds.");
        if (result.Report != null && File.Exists(result.Report)) throw new ArgumentException("Choose a new report path; refusing to reuse old evidence.");
        return result;
    }
    public void Apply(ViewerOptions options)
    {
        options.Simulation = options.Simulation with { Ships = Ships ?? options.Simulation.Ships, Aircraft = Aircraft ?? options.Simulation.Aircraft, Dense = Dense || options.Simulation.Dense };
        if (NoBasemap) options.Basemap.Enabled = false;
    }
}
public sealed record ProcessSample(double Seconds, long PrivateBytes, long WorkingSetBytes, long ManagedBytes, long AllocatedBytes, double CpuSeconds,
    int Gen0, int Gen1, int Gen2, int Tracked, int RetainedPoints, int Mailbox, int RenderedSymbols, int RenderedTraces);

public static class BenchmarkReport
{
    public static void Write(string path, MainWindow window, IReadOnlyList<ProcessSample> samples, double duration, string coverage, string? error = null, IReadOnlyList<string>? checks = null)
    {
        var vm = window.ViewModel; var current = vm.Current; var latency = vm.Metrics.IngestionToRenderMs.Summary(); var ui = vm.Metrics.DispatcherMs.Summary();
        var warm = samples.Where(s => s.Seconds >= 600).ToArray();
        double? memorySlope = null;
        if (warm.Length >= 3)
        {
            var x = warm.Average(s => s.Seconds); var y = warm.Average(s => (double)s.PrivateBytes);
            var denominator = warm.Sum(s => Math.Pow(s.Seconds - x, 2));
            memorySlope = denominator > 0 ? warm.Sum(s => (s.Seconds - x) * (s.PrivateBytes - y)) / denominator * 60 / 1048576 : 0;
        }
        bool? latencyPass = latency.WindowSamples > 0 ? latency.LifetimeP95UpperBound <= 3000 && latency.LifetimeP99UpperBound <= 5000 : null;
        bool? uiPass = ui.WindowSamples > 0 ? ui.LifetimeP95UpperBound <= 250 : null;
        var boundsPass = samples.Count > 0 && samples.All(s => s.Tracked <= vm.Options.Simulation.Population && s.Mailbox <= vm.Options.Simulation.Population &&
            (long)s.RetainedPoints <= (long)vm.Options.Simulation.Population * vm.Options.History.MaximumPoints);
        var allSymbolsObserved = samples.Any(s => s.RenderedSymbols == vm.Options.Simulation.Population);
        var fullPopulation = samples.Any(s => s.Tracked == vm.Options.Simulation.Population);
        var soakEvaluated = duration >= 1800 && memorySlope != null;
        var basemapReady = !vm.Options.Basemap.Enabled || vm.Metrics.BasemapImageResponses > 0;
        var basemapHealthy = !vm.Options.Basemap.Enabled || basemapReady && vm.Metrics.BasemapFailures == 0;
        var dpi = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(window.MapView);
        var report = new
        {
            schemaVersion = 1, success = error == null && vm.Engine?.Failure == null && fullPopulation && vm.Renderer.Frames > 0,
            error = error ?? vm.Engine?.Failure?.Message, utc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, durationSeconds = duration, coverage, checks,
            options = vm.Options, tracesEnabled = vm.ShowTraces,
            environment = new { os = Environment.OSVersion.ToString(), runtime = Environment.Version.ToString(), processors = Environment.ProcessorCount,
                machine = Environment.MachineName, cpu = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PROCESSOR_IDENTIFIER"), mapsui = typeof(Map).Assembly.GetName().Version?.ToString(),
                width = window.MapView.ActualWidth, height = window.MapView.ActualHeight, dpiX = dpi.PixelsPerInchX, dpiY = dpi.PixelsPerInchY,
                basemap = vm.BasemapStatus, cache = "Existing GeoServer cache; client cache starts empty per process. Server cache not flushed." },
            counts = current?.Source.Counts, eligible = current?.Visible.Length, renderedSymbols = vm.Renderer.RenderedSymbols, renderedTraces = vm.Renderer.RenderedTraces,
            frames = vm.Renderer.Frames, measurements = vm.Metrics.Summary(),
            acceptance = new { fullPopulation, allSymbolsObserved, basemapReady, basemapHealthy, boundedState = boundsPass, latencyPass, dispatcherPass = uiPass,
                soakEvaluated, memorySlopeMiBPerMinute = memorySlope, memoryStable = soakEvaluated ? memorySlope <= 1 : (bool?)null,
                performanceTargetsMet = latencyPass == true && uiPass == true && boundsPass && basemapHealthy && (!vm.Options.Simulation.Dense || allSymbolsObserved),
                note = "Success denotes functional completion. Performance targets and soak are separate. Memory stability uses <=1 MiB/min linear trend after minute 10; inspect CSV for steps/leaks. Acceptance uses bounded whole-run histograms (1 ms buckets; percentile overflow above 60 s is null/failure). Exact recent-window percentiles are also reported." },
            samples
        };
        path = Path.GetFullPath(path); Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(path)!);
        File.WriteAllText(path, JsonSerializer.Serialize(report, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
        using var writer = new StreamWriter(Path.ChangeExtension(path, ".csv"));
        writer.WriteLine("seconds,private_bytes,working_set_bytes,managed_bytes,allocated_bytes,cpu_seconds,gc0,gc1,gc2,tracked,history_points,mailbox,rendered_symbols,rendered_traces");
        foreach (var s in samples) writer.WriteLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"{s.Seconds:F3},{s.PrivateBytes},{s.WorkingSetBytes},{s.ManagedBytes},{s.AllocatedBytes},{s.CpuSeconds:F3},{s.Gen0},{s.Gen1},{s.Gen2},{s.Tracked},{s.RetainedPoints},{s.Mailbox},{s.RenderedSymbols},{s.RenderedTraces}"));
    }
}
public static class DesktopRun
{
    public static async Task RunAsync(MainWindow window, RunOptions options)
    {
        var vm = window.ViewModel; var samples = new List<ProcessSample>(); var checks = new List<string>(); string? error = null;
        var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        using var process = Process.GetCurrentProcess();
        try
        {
            vm.ShowTraces = !options.NoTraces; await vm.StartCommand.ExecuteAsync(null);
            var nextSample = 0d; var nextInteraction = 15d; var interaction = 0;
            while (watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds < options.Seconds)
            {
                await Task.Delay(250);
                if (vm.Engine?.Failure != null) throw new InvalidOperationException("Pipeline failure", vm.Engine.Failure);
                if (watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds >= nextSample)
                {
                    nextSample += Math.Max(1, options.Seconds / 720d); process.Refresh(); var c = vm.Current?.Source.Counts;
                    samples.Add(new(watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds, process.PrivateMemorySize64, process.WorkingSet64, GC.GetTotalMemory(false), GC.GetTotalAllocatedBytes(),
                        process.TotalProcessorTime.TotalSeconds, GC.CollectionCount(0), GC.CollectionCount(1), GC.CollectionCount(2), c?.Tracked ?? 0, c?.HistoryPoints ?? 0,
                        c?.Pending ?? 0, vm.Renderer.RenderedSymbols, vm.Renderer.RenderedTraces));
                }
                if (options.Exercise && watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds >= nextInteraction && vm.Rows.Length > 0)
                {
                    nextInteraction += 10;
                    switch (interaction++ % 5)
                    {
                        case 0: vm.SelectKey(vm.Rows[0].Key); vm.FocusCommand.Execute(null); vm.Following = true; break;
                        case 1: vm.SuspendFollowing(); vm.HomeCommand.Execute(null); break;
                        case 2: vm.DisplayEditor.Search = "0001"; vm.ApplyDisplayFilterCommand.Execute(null); break;
                        case 3: vm.ClearDisplayFilterCommand.Execute(null); break;
                        case 4:
                            var p = vm.Map.Navigator.Viewport; vm.Map.Navigator.CenterOn(p.CenterX + p.Resolution * 20, p.CenterY, 0); break;
                    }
                }
            }
            void Check(bool condition, string label) { if (!condition) throw new InvalidOperationException(label); checks.Add(label); }
            Check(vm.Current != null && vm.Current.Source.Counts.Tracked == vm.Options.Simulation.Population, "Configured population is tracked");
            Check(vm.Renderer.Frames > 0 && window.MapView.ActualWidth > 0, "Native WPF map rendered frames");
            Check(options.NoBasemap || vm.Metrics.BasemapImageResponses > 0, "Basemap image responses received, or explicit overlay-only mode");
            Check(options.NoBasemap || vm.Metrics.BasemapFailures == 0, "Basemap loaded without request errors");
            if (options.Smoke)
            {
                vm.ClearDisplayFilterCommand.Execute(null); vm.HomeCommand.Execute(null); await Task.Delay(1500);
                var selected = vm.Rows.First(); vm.SelectKey(selected.Key); vm.FocusCommand.Execute(null); vm.Following = true; await Task.Delay(1500);
                Check(vm.Details.Contains(selected.Identity), "Selection details identify the track");
                selected = vm.Rows.First(t => t.Key == selected.Key);
                var point = OgcDemo.Core.Coordinates.Project(selected.Report.Longitude, selected.Report.Latitude);
                var screen = vm.Map.Navigator.Viewport.WorldToScreen(new MPoint(point.X, point.Y));
                Check(window.MapView.GetMapInfo(screen, [vm.Symbols]).Feature != null, "Rendered traffic symbol is pickable");
                Check(!vm.ShowTraces || vm.Current!.Traces.Features.Length > 0, "Trace geometry is published");
                vm.SuspendFollowing(); Check(!vm.Following, "Manual interaction suspends following");
                var before = vm.Engine!.Clock.Seconds; vm.PauseCommand.Execute(null); await Task.Delay(1200);
                Check(vm.Engine.Clock.Seconds - before < .2, "Pause freezes simulation time"); vm.ResumeCommand.Execute(null);
                vm.DisplayEditor.Ships = false; vm.ApplyDisplayFilterCommand.Execute(null); await Task.Delay(1800);
                Check(vm.Rows.All(t => t.Kind == TrackKind.Aircraft), "Display filter hides ships");
                Check(vm.Current!.Source.Counts.Tracked == vm.Options.Simulation.Population, "Display filtering preserves track state");
                vm.ClearDisplayFilterCommand.Execute(null); vm.InputEditor.Aircraft = false; vm.ApplyInputFilterCommand.Execute(null); await Task.Delay(1800);
                Check(vm.Current!.Source.Tracks.All(t => t.Kind == TrackKind.Ship), "Input filter purges aircraft");
                vm.ClearInputFilterCommand.Execute(null); await vm.ResetCommand.ExecuteAsync(null); await vm.StartCommand.ExecuteAsync(null);
                await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(Math.Max(vm.ShipInterval, vm.AircraftInterval) + 2));
                Check(vm.Current!.Source.Counts.Tracked == vm.Options.Simulation.Population, "Reset/start rebuilds the configured population");
                var original = vm.Options.Basemap.Url; vm.Options.Basemap.Url = "http://127.0.0.1:1/geoserver"; vm.BasemapProtocol = "WMTS";
                await vm.ConnectBasemap(); Check(vm.BasemapStatus.Contains("unavailable"), "Basemap failure is recoverable");
                vm.Options.Basemap.Url = original; vm.BasemapProtocol = options.NoBasemap ? "None" : "WMTS"; await vm.ConnectBasemap();
                vm.HomeCommand.Execute(null); await Task.Delay(1000);
            }
            Capture(window, Path.ChangeExtension(options.Report!, ".png"));
        }
        catch (Exception e) { error = e.ToString(); }
        BenchmarkReport.Write(options.Report!, window, samples, watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds,
            options.Smoke ? "Native WPF in-process smoke; no physical input automation." : options.Exercise ? "Native WPF benchmark with scripted navigation, following and display filtering." : "Native WPF fixed-view benchmark.", error, checks);
        window.Close();
    }
    private static void Capture(MainWindow window, string path)
    {
        var surface = (System.Windows.FrameworkElement)window.Content; surface.UpdateLayout();
        var image = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)surface.ActualWidth, (int)surface.ActualHeight, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); image.Render(surface);
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(image)); Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(path))!);
        using var stream = File.Create(path); encoder.Save(stream);
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-tests-trafficdemo-tests-traffictests-cs"></a>

### `tests/TrafficDemo.Tests/TrafficTests.cs`

Source SHA-256: `92448dfca1e59454059627fce0050958efa20573a631c0680b6c353c98200960`

``````csharp
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Layers;
using Mapsui.Nts;
using NetTopologySuite.Geometries;
using TrafficDemo.Core;
using Xunit;

namespace TrafficDemo.Tests;
public sealed class TrafficTests
{
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Epoch = new(2026, 10, 17, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
    private static TrackReport Report(long sequence = 1, double seconds = 0, TrackKind kind = TrackKind.Ship, string identity = "123") =>
        new(kind, identity, "SIM-TEST", Epoch.AddSeconds(seconds), sequence, 19, 38, 12, 90, kind == TrackKind.Aircraft ? 30000 : null);
    private static ReceivedReport Received(TrackReport report) => ReceivedReport.Now(report);
    [Fact] public void PopulationAboveTargetIsAllowedButWarned()
    {
        var o = new SimulationOptions { Ships = 15000, Aircraft = 5000 }; o.Validate(); Assert.Equal(20000, o.Population); Assert.Contains("Warning", o.Warning);
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => new SimulationOptions { Ships = -1 }.Validate());
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => new SimulationOptions { Ships = int.MaxValue, Aircraft = 1 }.Validate());
    }
    [Fact] public void SimulatorIsRepeatableAndMovementMatchesSpeed()
    {
        var o = new SimulationOptions { Ships = 10, Aircraft = 10 }; var a = new SimulatedFeed(o, new(1, Epoch)); var b = new SimulatedFeed(o, new(1, Epoch));
        for (var i = 0; i < 20; i++) Assert.Equal(a.ReportAt(i, 123, 5), b.ReportAt(i, 123, 5));
        var start = a.ReportAt(0, 0, 1); var end = a.ReportAt(0, 1, 2);
        Assert.NotEqual(start.Longitude, end.Longitude);
        var x = (end.Longitude - start.Longitude) * Math.Cos(start.Latitude * Math.PI / 180); var y = end.Latitude - start.Latitude;
        var distanceKm = Math.Sqrt(x * x + y * y) * 111.195;
        Assert.InRange(distanceKm, start.SpeedKnots * 1.852 / 3600 * .98, start.SpeedKnots * 1.852 / 3600 * 1.02);
        Assert.Null(start.AltitudeFeet); Assert.NotNull(a.ReportAt(19, 0, 1).AltitudeFeet);
    }
    [Fact] public void RouteTurnsWithoutTeleporting()
    {
        var route = new Route((19, 38), (19.1, 38)); var a = route.Position(route.LengthKm - .001); var b = route.Position(route.LengthKm + .001);
        Assert.Equal(a.Lon, b.Lon, 8); Assert.Equal(180, Math.Abs(a.Course - b.Course), 5);
    }
    [Fact] public async Task PauseFreezesTimeAndCancellationStopsFeed()
    {
        var clock = new SimulationClock(1, Epoch); var before = clock.Now; await Task.Delay(30); Assert.Equal(before, clock.Now);
        clock.SetPaused(false); await Task.Delay(30); Assert.True(clock.Now > before); clock.SetPaused(true);
        var paused = clock.Now; await Task.Delay(30); Assert.Equal(paused, clock.Now);
        using var cancel = new CancellationTokenSource(80); var feed = new SimulatedFeed(new() { Ships = 1, Aircraft = 0 }, clock);
        await Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync<OperationCanceledException>(async () => { await foreach (var _ in feed.ReadAsync(cancel.Token)) { } });
    }
    [Fact] public void MailboxRetainsNewestPerKeyAndEnforcesCapacity()
    {
        var m = new LatestReportMailbox(1);
        Assert.True(m.Offer(Received(Report()))); Assert.True(m.Offer(Received(Report(2, 2))));
        Assert.False(m.Offer(Received(Report(1, 1)))); Assert.False(m.Offer(Received(Report(identity: "456"))));
        Assert.False(m.Offer(Received(Report() with { Longitude = double.NaN })));
        var one = Assert.Single(m.Drain()); Assert.Equal(2, one.Report.Sequence); Assert.Equal(0, m.Count);
        var counts = m.Counters(); Assert.Equal(5, counts.Generated); Assert.Equal(1, counts.Coalesced); Assert.Equal(2, counts.Rejected); Assert.Equal(1, counts.CapacityRejected);
    }
    [Fact] public void StoreRejectsDuplicatesBackwardTimeAndMalformedReports()
    {
        var store = new TrackStore(new(), new()); Assert.True(store.Accept(Received(Report()), Epoch));
        Assert.False(store.Accept(Received(Report()), Epoch)); Assert.False(store.Accept(Received(Report(2, -1)), Epoch));
        Assert.False(store.Accept(Received(Report(2, 2) with { Course = 360 }), Epoch.AddSeconds(2)));
        Assert.False(store.Accept(Received(Report(2, 100)), Epoch)); Assert.Equal(1, store.Count); Assert.Equal(4, store.Rejected);
    }
    [Fact] public void HistoryIsBoundedByTimeAndCountAndKeepsGaps()
    {
        var store = new TrackStore(new() { MaximumPoints = 3, RetentionSeconds = 10, SampleSeconds = 2 }, new());
        for (var i = 0; i < 8; i++) store.Accept(Received(Report(i + 1, i * 2)), Epoch.AddSeconds(i * 2));
        var t = Assert.Single(store.Snapshot(Epoch.AddSeconds(14))); Assert.Equal(3, t.Trace.Length); Assert.Equal(Epoch.AddSeconds(10), t.Trace[0].Time);
        store.Accept(Received(Report(11, 16)), Epoch.AddSeconds(16)); Assert.True(store.Snapshot(Epoch.AddSeconds(16))[0].Trace[^1].BreakBefore);
        store.Age(Epoch.AddSeconds(25)); Assert.Single(store.Snapshot(Epoch.AddSeconds(25))[0].Trace);
        store.Age(Epoch.AddSeconds(50)); Assert.True(store.Snapshot(Epoch.AddSeconds(50))[0].Stale); Assert.Equal(0, store.HistoryPointCount);
        store.Age(Epoch.AddSeconds(136)); Assert.Equal(0, store.Count);
    }
    [Fact] public void TimeGapAndDatelineStartNewSegments()
    {
        var store = new TrackStore(new(), new()); store.Accept(Received(Report()), Epoch);
        store.Accept(Received(Report(2, 20)), Epoch.AddSeconds(20)); Assert.True(store.Snapshot(Epoch.AddSeconds(20))[0].Trace[^1].BreakBefore);
        store.Accept(Received(Report(3, 22) with { Longitude = -179 }), Epoch.AddSeconds(22));
        store.Accept(Received(Report(4, 24) with { Longitude = 179 }), Epoch.AddSeconds(24)); Assert.True(store.Snapshot(Epoch.AddSeconds(24))[0].Trace[^1].BreakBefore);
    }
    [Fact] public void FiltersSeparateAdmissionFromDisplayAndValidateBounds()
    {
        var store = new TrackStore(new(), new()); store.Accept(Received(Report()), Epoch); store.Accept(Received(Report(kind: TrackKind.Aircraft)), Epoch);
        var filter = new TrackFilter { Ships = false, MinimumAltitude = 20000, MaximumSpeed = 20, Bounds = new(18, 37, 20, 39) };
        Assert.Single(store.Snapshot(Epoch), t => filter.Matches(t.Report)); Assert.Equal(2, store.Count);
        store.SetInputFilter(filter); Assert.Equal(1, store.Count); Assert.False(store.Accept(Received(Report(2, 1)), Epoch.AddSeconds(1)));
        Assert.True(new GeoBounds(170, -10, -170, 10).Contains(179, 0)); Assert.False(new GeoBounds(170, -10, -170, 10).Contains(0, 0));
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => new TrackFilter { MinimumSpeed = 30, MaximumSpeed = 20 }.Validate());
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => new TrackFilter { Bounds = new(0, 50, 20, 10) }.Validate());
    }
    [Fact] public void IndexFindsCrossingTraceWithEndpointsOutsideViewport()
    {
        var factory = new GeometryFactory(); var line = new GeometryFeature(factory.CreateLineString([new(-100, 0), new(100, 0)]));
        var outside = new PointFeature(100, 0); var index = new FeatureIndex([line, outside]);
        Assert.Same(line, Assert.Single(index.Query(new MRect(-10, -10, 10, 10)))); Assert.Empty(index.Query(null));
    }
    [Fact] public void SnapshotDoesNotMutatePublishedFeaturesAndRetainsCrossingTrace()
    {
        var points = new[] { new TracePoint(Epoch, -100, 0, false), new TracePoint(Epoch.AddSeconds(2), 100, 0, false) };
        var track = new TrackView(Report(), 0, false, points); var source = new TrafficSnapshot(1, Epoch, [track], new(1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 2));
        var builder = new SnapshotBuilder(); var first = builder.Build(source, new(), 1, true);
        Assert.Single(first.Traces.Query(new MRect(-10, -10, 10, 10)));
        var initialExtent = first.Symbols.Features[0].Extent!.MinX;
        var second = builder.Build(source with { Tracks = [track with { Report = Report(2, 2) with { Longitude = 20 } }] }, new(), 1, true);
        Assert.Equal(initialExtent, first.Symbols.Features[0].Extent!.MinX); Assert.NotEqual(initialExtent, second.Symbols.Features[0].Extent!.MinX);
        var hidden = builder.Build(source, new() { Ships = false }, 1, true); Assert.Empty(hidden.Symbols.Features); Assert.Empty(hidden.Traces.Features);
        var restored = builder.Build(source, new(), 1, true); Assert.Single(restored.Traces.Features);
        var off = builder.Build(source, new(), 1, false); Assert.Empty(off.Traces.Features);
        var on = builder.Build(source, new(), 1, true); Assert.Single(on.Traces.Features);
    }
    [Fact] public void TraceGapsAreNotJoinedBySimplification()
    {
        var track = new TrackView(Report(), 0, false, [new(Epoch, 0, 0, false), new(Epoch.AddSeconds(2), 1, 0, false), new(Epoch.AddSeconds(4), 100, 0, true), new(Epoch.AddSeconds(6), 101, 0, false)]);
        var s = new TrafficSnapshot(1, Epoch, [track], new(1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 4));
        var r = new SnapshotBuilder().Build(s, new(), 100, true); var f = Assert.IsType<GeometryFeature>(Assert.Single(r.Traces.Features));
        Assert.Equal(2, f.Geometry!.NumGeometries);
    }
    [Fact] public void MetricWindowRemainsBounded()
    {
        var m = new MetricWindow(3); foreach (var i in Enumerable.Range(1, 10)) m.Add(i);
        var s = m.Summary(); Assert.Equal(3, s.WindowSamples); Assert.Equal(10, s.TotalSamples); Assert.Equal(9, s.Mean); Assert.Equal(10, s.P99); Assert.Equal(10, s.LifetimeP95UpperBound);
        m.Add(70000); Assert.Null(m.Summary().LifetimeP99UpperBound);
    }
    [Fact] public async Task EngineStopsCleanlyAfterDeliveringSnapshots()
    {
        var ready = new TaskCompletionSource<TrafficSnapshot>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        await using var engine = new TrafficEngine(new() { Ships = 5, Aircraft = 5, ShipIntervalSeconds = .1, AircraftIntervalSeconds = .1 }, new());
        engine.SnapshotReady += s => ready.TrySetResult(s); engine.Pause(false);
        var snapshot = await ready.Task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5)); Assert.Equal(10, snapshot.Counts.Tracked); Assert.Null(engine.Failure);
    }
    [Theory] [InlineData(1000)] [InlineData(5000)] [InlineData(10000)] [InlineData(12000)]
    public void LargePopulationRemainsIntactWithoutClustering(int population)
    {
        var o = new SimulationOptions { Ships = population / 2, Aircraft = population - population / 2, Dense = true };
        var feed = new SimulatedFeed(o, new(1, Epoch)); var store = new TrackStore(new(), o);
        for (var n = 0; n < 3; n++) for (var i = 0; i < population; i++) store.Accept(Received(feed.ReportAt(i, n * 5, n + 1)), Epoch.AddSeconds(n * 5));
        var source = new TrafficSnapshot(1, Epoch.AddSeconds(10), store.Snapshot(Epoch.AddSeconds(10)), new(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, store.Count, 0, store.HistoryPointCount));
        var prepared = new SnapshotBuilder().Build(source, new(), 100, true);
        Assert.Equal(population, prepared.Symbols.Features.Length); Assert.Equal(population, prepared.Traces.Features.Length); Assert.Equal(population * 3, store.HistoryPointCount);
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-scripts-benchmark-traffic-ps1"></a>

### `scripts/Benchmark-Traffic.ps1`

Source SHA-256: `7f34289c20ed8e4ff96e36389be9d7167768b3434dd9a53609f26d38159267da`

``````powershell
param(
    [string]$Executable = '.artifacts/traffic-publish/TrafficDemo.Wpf.exe',
    [string]$OutputDirectory = ('.artifacts/traffic/benchmark-' + (Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss')),
    [int]$Seconds = 60,
    [switch]$Soak,
    [switch]$FullMatrix
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-Location (Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent)
$exe = (Resolve-Path $Executable).Path
$output = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($OutputDirectory)
if (Test-Path $output) { throw 'Choose a new output directory; benchmark evidence is never overwritten.' }
New-Item -ItemType Directory $output | Out-Null
$cases = @()
foreach ($count in @(1000,5000,10000)) {
    foreach ($mix in @('ships','aircraft','mixed')) {
        foreach ($basemap in @($true,$false)) {
            $cases += [pscustomobject]@{ Count=$count; Mix=$mix; Basemap=$basemap; Dense=$true; Traces=$true; Seconds=$Seconds; Exercise=$true; Kind='matrix' }
        }
    }
}
if ($FullMatrix) {
    foreach ($count in @(1000,5000,10000)) {
        foreach ($mix in @('ships','aircraft','mixed')) {
            foreach ($dense in @($true,$false)) {
                foreach ($basemap in @($true,$false)) {
                    $cases += [pscustomobject]@{Count=$count; Mix=$mix; Basemap=$basemap; Dense=$dense; Traces=$false; Seconds=$Seconds; Exercise=$true; Kind='trace-comparison'}
                    if (!$dense) { $cases += [pscustomobject]@{Count=$count; Mix=$mix; Basemap=$basemap; Dense=$false; Traces=$true; Seconds=$Seconds; Exercise=$true; Kind='dispersed'} }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
if ($Soak) { $cases += [pscustomobject]@{Count=10000; Mix='mixed'; Basemap=$true; Dense=$true; Traces=$true; Seconds=1800; Exercise=$false; Kind='soak'} }
$results = @()
foreach ($case in $cases) {
    $ships = if ($case.Mix -eq 'ships') {$case.Count} elseif ($case.Mix -eq 'aircraft') {0} else {$case.Count/2}
    $aircraft = $case.Count - $ships
    $name = '{0}-{1}-{2}-map{3}-dense{4}-traces{5}' -f $case.Kind,$case.Count,$case.Mix,[int]$case.Basemap,[int]$case.Dense,[int]$case.Traces
    $report = Join-Path $output ($name + '.json')
    $arguments = @('--report',('"'+$report+'"'),'--seconds',"$($case.Seconds)",'--ships',"$ships",'--aircraft',"$aircraft")
    if ($case.Dense) {$arguments += '--dense'}
    if (!$case.Basemap) {$arguments += '--no-basemap'}
    if (!$case.Traces) {$arguments += '--no-traces'}
    if ($case.Exercise) {$arguments += '--exercise'}
    Write-Output "Running $name ($($case.Seconds) seconds)"
    $p = Start-Process -FilePath $exe -ArgumentList $arguments -PassThru
    $null = $p.Handle # Retain the native handle so Windows PowerShell preserves ExitCode.
    if (!$p.WaitForExit(($case.Seconds + 120)*1000)) { throw "Benchmark timed out: $name. Inspect that process before continuing." }
    if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw "Traffic process failed with exit code $($p.ExitCode): $name" }
    if (!(Test-Path $report)) { throw "No report from $name; check the runtime." }
    $result = Get-Content $report -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
    $results += [pscustomobject]@{ Case=$name; FunctionalSuccess=$result.success; Acceptance=$result.acceptance; Report=$report }
    $results | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8 | Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 (Join-Path $output 'summary.json')
    # Continue recording other cases even when performance targets fail.
    if (!$result.success) { Write-Warning "Functional failure in $name; see report." }
}
$results
``````

<a id="file-scripts-test-trafficdesktop-ps1"></a>

### `scripts/Test-TrafficDesktop.ps1`

Source SHA-256: `aae92938fe10590f4b20c87b18344792ca9da3ae573cc60468c570c8846267d0`

``````powershell
param([string]$Executable = '.artifacts/traffic-publish/TrafficDemo.Wpf.exe', [string]$Report = '.artifacts/traffic/desktop-smoke.json', [switch]$NoBasemap)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-Location (Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent)
$exe = (Resolve-Path $Executable).Path
$path = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Report)
if (Test-Path $path) { throw 'Choose a new report name.' }
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force (Split-Path $path -Parent) | Out-Null
$arguments = @('--report', ('"' + $path + '"'), '--seconds', '10', '--ships', '100', '--aircraft', '100', '--dense', '--smoke')
if ($NoBasemap) { $arguments += '--no-basemap' }
$p = Start-Process -FilePath $exe -ArgumentList $arguments -PassThru
$null = $p.Handle # Retain the native handle so Windows PowerShell preserves ExitCode.
if (!$p.WaitForExit(180000)) { throw 'Traffic smoke timed out; inspect the test window. No other process was stopped.' }
if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw "Traffic process exited with code $($p.ExitCode). Inspect native error logs and the report." }
if (!(Test-Path $path)) { throw 'No smoke report; check the .NET Windows Desktop runtime.' }
$result = Get-Content $path -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
if (!$result.success) { throw ('Traffic smoke failed: ' + $result.error) }
$result
``````

<a id="file-scripts-ensure-trafficbasemap-ps1"></a>

### `scripts/Ensure-TrafficBasemap.ps1`

Source SHA-256: `72a670a2aee88b6f25b7cf23ed91b12d2c81260ff530a5716db63b8b7c687014`

``````powershell
param(
    [string]$BaseUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:8602/geoserver',
    [string]$ContainerName = 'east-europe-bundle',
    [string]$WslDistribution = '',
    [int]$TimeoutSeconds = 180,
    [switch]$CheckOnly
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
function Test-TrafficBasemap {
    try {
        $result = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing -TimeoutSec 5 -Uri ($BaseUrl.TrimEnd('/') + '/wms?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities&version=1.3.0')
        $xml = New-Object System.Xml.XmlDocument
        $xml.XmlResolver = $null
        $xml.LoadXml($result.Content)
        return $xml.DocumentElement.LocalName -eq 'WMS_Capabilities' -and $result.Content.Contains('omt:dark-matter-en')
    } catch { return $false }
}
if (Test-TrafficBasemap) { Write-Output 'Existing OSM GeoServer is ready; reused without restart.'; exit 0 }
if ($CheckOnly) { throw 'OSM GeoServer is not ready.' }
$uri = [Uri]$BaseUrl
if (!$uri.IsLoopback -or $uri.Port -ne 8602) { throw 'Automatic start is restricted to the existing loopback port-8602 OSM deployment.' }
if ($WslDistribution) {
    # Only inspect/start the known existing container. No imports, volume recreation or machine changes.
    $name = if ($ContainerName -eq 'east-europe-bundle') { 'east-europe-bundle_geoserver_1' } else { $ContainerName }
    $ports = & wsl.exe --distribution $WslDistribution --exec podman inspect --format '{{json .HostConfig.PortBindings}}' $name 2>$null
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or !(($ports | ConvertFrom-Json).'8080/tcp' | Where-Object HostPort -eq '8602')) { throw 'The selected WSL deployment does not expose the expected port. Verify the container and distribution.' }
    & wsl.exe --distribution $WslDistribution --exec podman start $name | Out-Null
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Starting the existing WSL GeoServer failed.' }
} else {
    $podman = Get-Command podman.exe -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if (!$podman) { throw 'GeoServer is unavailable. Supply -WslDistribution for a WSL deployment, or start the prepared osm-basemap-demo bundle using its documented launcher.' }
    $ports = & $podman.Source inspect --format '{{json .HostConfig.PortBindings}}' $ContainerName 2>$null
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or !(($ports | ConvertFrom-Json).'8080/tcp' | Where-Object HostPort -eq '8602')) { throw 'The selected Windows Podman container does not expose port 8602. For WSL, supply -WslDistribution and the existing container name.' }
    & $podman.Source start $ContainerName | Out-Null
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Starting the existing GeoServer failed.' }
}
$deadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds($TimeoutSeconds)
do {
    if (Test-TrafficBasemap) { Write-Output 'OSM GeoServer is ready.'; exit 0 }
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
} while ([DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $deadline)
throw 'GeoServer startup timed out. Inspect the OSM deployment logs; the viewer can still run without a basemap.'
``````

<a id="file-scripts-ensure-traffic-basemap-sh"></a>

### `scripts/ensure-traffic-basemap.sh`

Source SHA-256: `9cf340f14e648381e314d83bc0892fca24716edb51e2aebe869ecc3e6f89891b`

``````text
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# Reuse the existing small-gis-demos deployment; never provision or replace its volumes.
base_url="${TRAFFIC_BASEMAP_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8602/geoserver}"
container="${TRAFFIC_BASEMAP_CONTAINER:-east-europe-bundle_geoserver_1}"
engine="${TRAFFIC_CONTAINER_ENGINE:-podman}"
ready() {
  curl --fail --silent --max-time 5 "$base_url/wms?service=WMS&request=GetCapabilities&version=1.3.0" |
    python3 -c 'import sys,xml.etree.ElementTree as E; r=E.parse(sys.stdin).getroot(); sys.exit(0 if r.tag.endswith("WMS_Capabilities") and any(e.text=="omt:dark-matter-en" for e in r.iter()) else 1)' 2>/dev/null
}
if ready; then echo 'Existing OSM GeoServer is ready; reused without restart.'; exit 0; fi
if [[ "${1:-}" == '--check-only' ]]; then echo 'OSM GeoServer is not ready.' >&2; exit 1; fi
if [[ "$base_url" != 'http://127.0.0.1:8602/geoserver' ]]; then echo 'Automatic start requires the known loopback port-8602 deployment.' >&2; exit 1; fi
ports="$("$engine" inspect --format '{{json .HostConfig.PortBindings}}' "$container")"
if ! python3 -c 'import json,sys; p=json.loads(sys.argv[1]); sys.exit(0 if any(str(x.get("HostPort"))=="8602" for x in p.get("8080/tcp",[]) or []) else 1)' "$ports"; then echo 'Existing container does not expose port 8602; verify deployment.' >&2; exit 1; fi
"$engine" start "$container" >/dev/null
for (( attempt=0; attempt<90; attempt++ )); do
  if ready; then echo 'OSM GeoServer is ready.'; exit 0; fi
  sleep 2
done
echo 'GeoServer startup timed out. Inspect deployment logs; overlay-only mode remains available.' >&2
exit 1
``````

<a id="file-scripts-start-trafficdemo-ps1"></a>

### `scripts/Start-TrafficDemo.ps1`

Source SHA-256: `5eaba9f1a89a12682367c70fd0ad14f3b966214355c21b9b0e20488bd83317e5`

``````powershell
param([string]$Executable = '.artifacts/traffic-publish/TrafficDemo.Wpf.exe', [string]$WslDistribution = '', [switch]$SkipBasemapCheck)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-Location (Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent)

Start-Process -FilePath $exe -WorkingDirectory (Split-Path $exe -Parent)
``````
