### `docs/validation.md`

Source SHA-256: `b33d8888c1d3ffcfa7a3a1cc5b636597f4e237c4be0ebec3d3d614281e4f9680`

``````text
# Validation record — 8 October 2026

## Verified results

| Check | Result | Evidence retained locally |
|---|---|---|
| Native Windows locked restore | Passed with the explicit development-validation feed | `.artifacts/windows/restore-windows.log` |
| Native Windows Release solution build | Passed, zero warnings and zero errors | `.artifacts/windows/build-windows.log` |
| Core and real loopback OGC fixture tests on Windows | **49 passed**, zero failed/skipped | `.artifacts/windows/windows-tests.trx` and `test-windows.log` |
| Framework-dependent win-x64 publish | Passed | `.artifacts/windows/publish-windows.log`; binaries in `.artifacts/publish` |
| Published Windows application integration smoke | **16 checks passed** | `.artifacts/windows/desktop-release.json` |
| Actual WPF render exported during smoke run | Inspected: points, line strokes, filled polygons, a polygon hole, selection highlight | `.artifacts/windows/desktop-release.png` |
| Generated documentation/source consistency | Passed on Linux and Windows; normalized LF output | `.artifacts/docs-check.log` and `.artifacts/windows/docs-windows.log` |
| Manual source listing comparison | All source blocks matched their current files after newline normalization | `.artifacts/manual-source-check.json` |

Windows validation used SDK 10.0.401, runtime 10.0.12, Windows x64 (OS version 10.0.26300). The SDK was extracted to a task-specific temporary directory after verifying its official SHA-512 hash; it was not installed system-wide. Linux cross-builds and earlier core tests also ran, but the table reports the final native Windows results. The Linux host lacked ICU, so early Linux tooling used invariant globalization; the native Windows tests did not use that workaround.

## What the tests exercise

Tests cover known-coordinate projection round trips, invalid coordinates, WMS/WFS axis order, JSON configuration binding, GeoJSON projection and polygon holes, geometry-specific styles, independent highlighting, layer order, stale-generation rejection, safe log behavior, and the supported GML geometry subset in both 3.1 and 3.2 encodings.

The fixture is a real loopback HTTP listener with synthetic XML, GML, and valid PNG responses. Protocol tests exercise both WMS versions, WMTS source selection and tile retrieval, both WFS versions, DescribeFeatureType, BBOX/feature limits, authentication on discovery and data requests, wrong CRS, malformed XML, service exceptions, HTTP errors, redirect rejection, header/body timeouts, oversized fixed-length and chunked responses, cancellation, and cross-origin rejection. Application configuration permits HTTPS only; loopback HTTP is explicitly enabled by the test harness.

The desktop smoke check creates a real WPF window. It verifies rendered point picking, line-interior picking, polygon-fill picking, selection/details/highlight behavior, clear-selection, visibility, opacity, layer ordering, addition/removal, and recovery from an unavailable service. It runs against the **published** application, not just the compiler output.

## Boundaries and remaining acceptance work

- **Manual physical-input walkthrough: pending.** The Computer Use runtime rejected this WSL workspace path (`sandboxCwd is not a local file URI`). Desktop coverage here is an in-process integration test plus inspection of its exported rendering. File-dialog interaction, physical mouse/keyboard navigation, display scaling, and accessibility still need the human checklist in lesson 11.
- **Internal Artifactory: not tested.** No internal endpoint was supplied. Development validation used an explicit ignored public NuGet configuration. The distributed configuration remains Artifactory-only with no fallback; lock files were preserved during the native Windows restore.
- **Actual internal OGC services and PKI: not tested.** No work URLs, credentials, or datasets were supplied. Fixtures establish the documented client behavior, not compatibility with every server, application schema, or enterprise certificate/proxy configuration.
- **Self-contained publishing: documented option, not the validated output.** The verified package is framework-dependent and needs the .NET 10 Desktop Runtime. Installers, signing, enterprise authentication beyond Basic, and production performance qualification remain extensions.

No work-server state was changed. Temporary build tools, binaries, logs, and reports are ignored by source control. Real credentials and private settings are not included in the project or generated manual.

## Traffic Lab validation — 2026-10-17

The traffic viewer is a separate application; the earlier OGC results above remain
historical evidence for that application. New traffic evidence is retained locally in
`.artifacts/traffic/`, with native Windows run reports, CSV samples and captured PNGs
under `.artifacts/traffic/windows/`. The test workstation was verified as an AMD Ryzen
9 9950X3D (16 cores / 32 logical processors), with approximately 61.3 GiB usable RAM,
Windows 10.0.26300, .NET 10.0.12 and Mapsui 5.1.0. The measured map viewport was
approximately 1106 ×607 WPF logical pixels at 120 DPI. These are workstation-specific
results, not a guarantee for other hardware or arbitrary history/report-rate settings.

### Build and functional checks

- Linux cross-build and native Windows Release build/publish succeeded. Core tests
  cover 18 traffic scenarios, including unclustered 12,000-object data. The existing
  OGC tests passed; an additional WMTS resource-selection regression raises that
  suite from 49 to 50 tests.
- Native smoke verified rendered symbols and picking, trace publication, selected
  details, following suspension, pause, separate input/display filters, reset/start,
  and recoverable service failure. The process exited cleanly after the shutdown fix.
  These are in-process checks, not physical mouse/keyboard automation.
- The running Eastern Europe OSM GeoServer was reused. Both Windows and WSL could
  fetch its capabilities; the corrected WMTS integration received actual map images.
  The helper's healthy-service path and script syntax were checked. The stopped-server
  start path was not exercised against the live service; it was unnecessary to stop it.
- Visual inspection of native captures confirmed directional cyan ships, amber
  aircraft, the OSM basemap and the population table. Overlap in the dense scenario
  is intentional; counts are not reduced by clustering.

The 30-second dense 10,000-object check with traces and GeoServer recorded all 10,000
symbols, zero coalesced/capacity-rejected reports, zero basemap failures, p95/p99
whole-run ingestion-to-render upper bounds of **1.131 / 1.212 seconds**, p95 render
upper bound **127 ms**, and p95 dispatcher upper bound **102 ms**. All applicable
short-run targets passed. This short run alone does **not** establish soak stability.

### Integration defects found during validation

The implementation was corrected using actual native/service evidence:

1. GeoServer advertises an empty WMTS style identifier; BruTile normalizes it to
   `default`, so the viewer selects that normalized value.
2. GeoServer also advertises TileJSON/FeatureInfo resources. BruTile 6 could select
   those templates as tile requests, producing incomplete basemaps. The shared adapter
   now passes only tile resources for the configured image format to the parser.
   A fixture regression verifies that auxiliary endpoints are never requested as tiles.
3. A synchronously completed disposal could reenter the WPF Closing event. Final
   close is now dispatched after the event returns; runners check process exit codes.

Earlier diagnostic runs with missing tiles or failed shutdown are not final acceptance
results. Their existence is not evidence of a passing GeoServer integration.

Native execution used a Windows-local source copy and a portable SDK. A copied
cross-built DLL was rejected by this workstation's Application Control during an
intermediate attempt; the native Windows build ran successfully. No security settings
were changed. Use the documented Windows build workflow when reproducing these results.

### Completed 30-minute soak

The native Release fixed-view run used 5,000 ships plus 5,000 aircraft, dense mode,
normal 5 s / 2 s reporting, five-minute / 150-point histories, traces enabled and the
live GeoServer WMTS basemap. It completed in 1,800.17 seconds and exited with code 0.
Evidence: `.artifacts/traffic/windows/soak-10k-1800s.{json,csv,png}`.

| Whole-run measurement | Result |
| --- | ---: |
| Generated and accepted reports | 6,224,391 each |
| Rejected / coalesced / capacity-rejected reports | 0 / 0 / 0 |
| Tracked and dense-view symbols observed | 10,000 / 10,000 |
| Retained history points at completion | 1,036,453 |
| Ingestion-to-render p95 / p99 upper bounds | 1,259 / 1,333 ms |
| Dispatcher p95 upper bound | 111 ms |
| Mapsui render p95 upper bound | 136 ms |
| Snapshot preparation p95 upper bound | 155 ms |
| UI publication p95 upper bound | 6 ms |
| Displayed-report age p95 / p99 upper bounds | 4.77 / 5.17 s |
| Basemap image responses / request failures | 32 / 0 |
| Replaced pending snapshots | 0 |
| Private memory at completion | 489.7 MiB |
| Private-memory trend after minute 10 | +0.539 MiB/min |

All recorded bounds and latency targets passed. The memory trend passed the explicit
1 MiB/min diagnostic threshold; post-minute-10 private bytes ranged approximately
470–521 MiB and fluctuated around a plateau. This does not prove the absence of every
possible leak. Displayed-report age includes the five-second ship cadence and is
separate from ingestion latency. Stored history was not disabled; simplification
reduces the displayed vertices independently of the retained-point count.

The application allocated about 164 GiB cumulatively over this run (roughly 93 MiB/s),
while retained process memory stayed bounded. This distinguishes allocation churn
from a retention leak and is a useful optimization target for future work. The run
used about 601 CPU seconds over 1,800 wall seconds. No other traffic benchmark was
run concurrently. Server cache state was retained; the application began with an
empty client cache. The PNG shows all symbols submitted in the dense viewport, with
intentional heavy overlap along the synthetic corridors.

### Population/type and basemap comparisons

All **18 native Release cases passed** functional checks, full-population/dense-view
checks, latency/dispatcher targets, bounded-state checks and basemap health. Each
case ran for 30 seconds in a fresh process, with dense mode and traces enabled.
The early interaction steps select/follow a track and return home. The full interaction
cycle is checked separately in a longer run. The committed runner defaults to
60 seconds; this recorded comparison explicitly used `-Seconds 30`.

Evidence: `.artifacts/traffic/windows/matrix/`, including `summary.json` and separate
JSON/CSV/PNG files for every case. Values below are conservative whole-run histogram
upper bounds in milliseconds. The dispatcher column is the worse p95 of the two
basemap modes for that row.

| Objects | Population | GeoServer ingestion p95 / p99 | Overlay ingestion p95 / p99 | Dispatcher p95 max |
| ---: | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 1,000 | ships | 1085 / 3739 | 1085 / 3513 | 14 |
| 1,000 | aircraft | 1116 / 1775 | 1071 / 1572 | 15 |
| 1,000 | mixed | 1077 / 1427 | 1077 / 1426 | 9 |
| 5,000 | ships | 1148 / 3832 | 1157 / 3920 | 53 |
| 5,000 | aircraft | 1183 / 1865 | 1185 / 1801 | 46 |
| 5,000 | mixed | 1116 / 1862 | 1138 / 2122 | 44 |
| 10,000 | ships | 1217 / 4013 | 1209 / 3946 | 104 |
| 10,000 | aircraft | 1231 / 1907 | 1234 / 1889 | 97 |
| 10,000 | mixed | 1158 / 1975 | 1191 / 1994 | 130 |

The all-ships interaction p99 is higher than the fixed-view soak: returning home
introduces reports that were offscreen during the focused view, with a five-second
source cadence. These samples remain included. No objects or traces were silently
removed to pass a target. Short comparison runs do not replace the 30-minute soak.

### Additional views, traces and interaction checks

These final native Release runs all passed functional and applicable performance
checks. Values are whole-run conservative upper bounds in milliseconds. Each is a
fresh process. The six 20-second 10k comparisons complete the dense/dispersed,
traces-on/off and GeoServer/overlay combinations for the mixed 10,000-object case;
they are not a claim that the optional full 72-case Cartesian matrix was executed.

| Case / evidence stem | Seconds | Ingestion p95 / p99 | Dispatcher p95 | Result |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
| dense-10k-interactions | 65.3 | 1267 / 3378 | 115 | Pass |
| dispersed-10k-map | 20.1 | 1126 / 1212 | 130 | Pass |
| dispersed-10k-overlay | 20.1 | 1188 / 1264 | 120 | Pass |
| dense-10k-no-traces-map | 20.1 | 1141 / 1209 | 121 | Pass |
| dense-10k-no-traces-overlay | 20.1 | 1148 / 1209 | 74 | Pass |
| dispersed-10k-no-traces-map | 20.2 | 1176 / 1215 | 84 | Pass |
| dispersed-10k-no-traces-overlay | 20.1 | 1135 / 1195 | 72 | Pass |
| dense-12k-map | 20.3 | 1203 / 1248 | 135 | Pass |

The 65-second interaction run includes selection/following, zoom-to-object, home,
display filtering, restoring the full display, and panning. The 12,000-object run
tracked and drew all 12,000 objects; the visible warning did not impose a 10,000 cap.
The native functional smoke (`smoke-final.json`) passed **14 checks** and exited 0.
Its deliberate pause/reset and failed-service injection make its latency and basemap
health acceptance fields fail; it is not a steady-load performance acceptance run.
The baseline comparisons and soak above have zero basemap failures.

Final Windows build: **zero warnings/errors; 68 tests passed** (18 traffic, 50 OGC).
The final WPF binary includes an additional startup-only guard that refuses to overwrite
an existing benchmark report even through the error path. The soak and baseline matrix
predate that guard; tracking, rendering, measurement and OGC logic are unchanged.
The core DLL hashes are identical; source/binary hashes and build/test evidence are
retained in `run-manifest.json` and the native logs/TRX files.

The final live **WMS** check (1,000 objects, regional view, 20 seconds) also passed
with image responses and zero basemap failures (`wms-final.json`). The original OGC
application's newly published native smoke passed **all 16 checks** (`ogc-final.json`).
A separate startup regression preserved an existing report byte-for-byte and returned
the expected refusal exit code 1 (`report-preservation-check.json`).

Generated documentation and full source listings are checked against the current
files. Private configuration, binaries and machine-specific reports remain ignored;
the unrelated pre-existing `docs/nuget-packages.md` was not changed. Physical
mouse/keyboard/file-dialog testing remains unverified because the available Computer
Use runtime rejected this WSL workspace path. Programmatic native interactions and
rendered captures are the evidence reported here. The live GeoServer was not stopped
to exercise the optional startup path.
``````

<a id="file-src-hellomap-wpf-mainwindow-xaml-cs"></a>

### `src/HelloMap.Wpf/MainWindow.xaml.cs`

Source SHA-256: `c495cd21a683536b10f69e93efe75f8b44e1f3d94d982650737d6ff3d5165141`

``````csharp
using System.Windows;
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Layers;
using Mapsui.Nts;
using Mapsui.Projections;
using Mapsui.Providers.Wms;
using Mapsui.Styles;
using NetTopologySuite.Geometries;

namespace HelloMap.Wpf;

public partial class MainWindow : Window
{
    // Change these two values to your WMS endpoint and advertised layer name.
    private const string WmsUrl = "https://ogc.example.invalid/wms";
    private const string WmsLayer = "demo:basemap";
    private readonly ImageLayer _basemap = new("WMS basemap");

    public MainWindow()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        // Longitude/latitude vertices near Tel Aviv, projected to the map's CRS.
        var vertices = new[] { (34.78, 32.03), (34.82, 32.08), (34.87, 32.05), (34.92, 32.10) }
            .Select(vertex =>
            {
                var (x, y) = SphericalMercator.FromLonLat(vertex.Item1, vertex.Item2);
                return new Coordinate(x, y);
            }).ToArray();

        var line = new GeometryFeature { Geometry = new LineString(vertices) };
        var overlay = new MemoryLayer("Polyline with vertices")
        {
            Features = new[] { line },
            Style = new StyleCollection
            {
                Styles =
                {
                    new VectorStyle { Line = new Pen(Color.Red, 3) },
                    // SymbolStyle draws a circle at each vertex of the same geometry.
                    new SymbolStyle { Fill = new Brush(Color.White), Outline = new Pen(Color.Red, 2), SymbolScale = 0.5 }
                }
            }
        };

        var map = new Map { CRS = "EPSG:3857" };
        map.Layers.Add(_basemap); // First layer is underneath.
        map.Layers.Add(overlay);
        map.Navigator.ZoomToBox(overlay.Extent!.Grow(2000));
        MapView.Map = map;
        Closed += (_, _) => MapView.Dispose();
    }

    private async void WindowLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
    {
        Loaded -= WindowLoaded;
        try
        {
            var wms = await WmsProvider.CreateAsync(WmsUrl, "1.3.0");
            wms.CRS = "EPSG:3857";
            wms.AddLayer(WmsLayer);
            wms.SetImageFormat("image/png");
            if (!IsLoaded) return;
            _basemap.DataSource = wms;
            MapView.RefreshData();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            if (IsLoaded)
                MessageBox.Show(this, "Could not load the WMS basemap. Check WmsUrl and WmsLayer.\n" + ex.Message, "Hello Map");
        }
    }
}
``````

<a id="file-src-hellomap-wpf-readme-md"></a>

### `src/HelloMap.Wpf/README.md`

Source SHA-256: `8e4e838aec70b5990610208fe16737a46cf7b2ee5eb6ab3a702f671210e4b8f1`

``````text
# Hello Map: a minimal WPF demo

One window, one Mapsui map control, one WMS basemap, and one polyline layer with four visible vertex markers. Map setup lives in [MainWindow.xaml.cs](MainWindow.xaml.cs); the window layout is [MainWindow.xaml](MainWindow.xaml). This example uses ordinary code-behind, without MVVM, dependency injection, or a configuration framework.

## Configure and run

Use Windows with the .NET 10 SDK. Run the commands below from the **repository root**; the project also uses the root `global.json` and `Directory.Build.props`.

1. In `MainWindow.xaml.cs`, replace `WmsUrl` and `WmsLayer` with your internal WMS endpoint and its advertised layer name. The checked-in URL is a placeholder. The service must support anonymous WMS 1.3.0, `EPSG:3857`, and `image/png`.
2. The four `(longitude, latitude)` pairs describe a line near Tel Aviv. Change them if your service covers another area. The app projects them to Web Mercator and zooms to the line.
3. If you have not already configured a private NuGet feed, copy and edit the root configuration:

```powershell
Copy-Item NuGet.Config NuGet.local.Config
notepad NuGet.local.Config
# Replace the example Artifactory URL with your internal NuGet v3 feed.
```

Restore and run:

```powershell
dotnet restore src/HelloMap.Wpf --configfile NuGet.local.Config --locked-mode
dotnet run --project src/HelloMap.Wpf --no-restore
```

Drag to pan; use the mouse wheel to zoom. The red polyline and white vertex circles appear immediately. The WMS basemap loads asynchronously underneath them. If loading WMS capabilities fails, the app displays an error and keeps the polyline usable. Seeing the basemap requires a reachable service with data covering the line.

## Why does such a small demo have 36 NuGet packages?

The [project file](HelloMap.Wpf.csproj) declares only **two direct package references**, both version **5.1.0**:

| Direct package | What this demo uses |
| --- | --- |
| `Mapsui.Wpf` | The WPF `MapControl` and its mapping/rendering dependencies. |
| `Mapsui.Nts` | `GeometryFeature`, which connects a NetTopologySuite `LineString` to a Mapsui layer. |

Those libraries declare their own dependencies. NuGet follows those declarations recursively, producing **34 transitive packages**, for **36 packages total** in the current [packages.lock.json](packages.lock.json). These counts describe this checked-in version and target framework; upgrades can change them.

“Direct” means explicitly listed in the project file, not “called by our code.” For example, this code uses types from `Mapsui` and `NetTopologySuite`, although both arrive transitively. `Mapsui.Nts` also arrives through `Mapsui.Rendering.Skia`; its explicit reference documents this demo's geometry dependency. Removing that reference alone would not remove it from the dependency tree.

| Transitive package or family | Why it appears |
| --- | --- |
| `Mapsui`, `NetTopologySuite` | Map, WMS provider, layers, styling, projection, and line/coordinate types used by the demo. |
| `Mapsui.Rendering.Skia`, `SkiaSharp`, `SkiaSharp.Views.WPF`, `SkiaSharp.Views.Desktop.Common` | Mapsui's drawing engine and WPF integration. |
| `Mapsui.Tiling`, `BruTile` | Tiling support included by `Mapsui.Wpf`. This demo uses an untiled WMS `ImageLayer`. |
| `NetTopologySuite.IO.GeoJSON4STJ`, `NetTopologySuite.Features` | GeoJSON/feature support included upstream. This demo constructs its line in code and does not read GeoJSON. |
| `Svg.Skia`, `Svg.Model`, `Svg.Custom`, `ShimSkiaSharp`, `ExCSS` | SVG and styling support included by the rendering stack. The demo supplies no custom SVG assets. |
| `HarfBuzzSharp`, `SkiaSharp.HarfBuzz`, `Topten.RichTextKit` | Text shaping and rich-text support included by the rendering stack. |
| `OpenTK`, `OpenTK.GLWpfControl`, and the other `OpenTK.*` packages | Dependencies brought in through `SkiaSharp.Views.WPF`. The OpenTK dependency tree includes graphics, input, windowing, compute, and audio components; the demo itself does not use audio or compute APIs. |
| `SkiaSharp.NativeAssets.*`, `HarfBuzzSharp.NativeAssets.*` | Platform-specific native binaries declared upstream. Linux entries can appear in this Windows project's lock file; Linux native libraries do not execute on Windows. |

The lock file records the **resolved dependency graph**, including versions and package hashes. It is not a trace of code executed at runtime, nor a list of files necessarily loaded by Windows. Asset selection depends on the target framework and runtime; rendering may also use library features internally.

## Restoring in an air-gapped environment

Your internal feed or prepared NuGet cache must provide the complete resolved package graph, including transitive packages. Mirroring only `Mapsui.Wpf` and `Mapsui.Nts` is insufficient. Windows/.NET targeting packs must also be available as required by the installed SDK; the lock file is not a complete offline SDK installation manifest.

Keep `packages.lock.json` under source control. `--locked-mode` checks that restore agrees with the committed dependency graph. Do not delete entries by hand to make the list shorter: upstream dependencies still request those packages. To change dependencies, edit package references deliberately, restore to regenerate the lock file, then review and validate the result.

Inspect the resolved package list after restoring, from the repository root:

```powershell
dotnet list src/HelloMap.Wpf/HelloMap.Wpf.csproj package --include-transitive --no-restore
```

For authentication, other OGC protocols, and layer controls, see the [full application README](../../README.md).
``````

<a id="file-tests-ogcdemo-tests-coretests-cs"></a>

### `tests/OgcDemo.Tests/CoreTests.cs`

Source SHA-256: `7495e1648b7f17e3cbd4d918724f79452724f15b76116010a0bf9fbc65ff6a76`

``````csharp
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Coordinates = OgcDemo.Core.Coordinates;
using System.Text;
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Layers;
using Mapsui.Nts;
using Mapsui.Projections;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using NetTopologySuite.Geometries;
using OgcDemo.Core;
using Xunit;

namespace OgcDemo.Tests;

public class CoreTests
{
    [Fact] public void ConfigurationDoesNotAppendDuplicateExtentDefaults()
    {
        using var input=new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("{\"Demo\":{\"InitialExtent\":[34.7,31.9,35.1,32.2]}}"));
        var config=new Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration.ConfigurationBuilder().AddJsonStream(input).Build();
        var options=config.GetSection("Demo").Get<DemoOptions>()!;
        Assert.Equal(4,options.InitialExtent.Length);
        Assert.True(Coordinates.ProjectExtent(options.InitialExtent).Width>0);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0,0)] [InlineData(34.82,32.07)] [InlineData(-123.5,-40.2)]
    public void ProjectionRoundtrip(double lon, double lat)
    {
        var p = Coordinates.Project(lon, lat); var ll = SphericalMercator.ToLonLat(p.X,p.Y);
        Assert.Equal(lon, ll.lon, 7); Assert.Equal(lat,ll.lat,7);
    }
    [Theory]
    [InlineData(200,0)] [InlineData(0,90)] [InlineData(double.NaN,0)]
    public void InvalidCoordinatesAreRejected(double lon,double lat) => Assert.Throws<DemoException>(() => Coordinates.Project(lon,lat));
    [Theory]
    [InlineData("WMS","1.3.0","20,10,40,30")]
    [InlineData("WMS","1.1.1","10,20,30,40")]
    [InlineData("WFS","2.0.0","20,10,40,30")]
    public void GeographicAxisOrder(string protocol,string version,string expected) => Assert.Equal(expected,Coordinates.Bbox(new MRect(10,20,30,40),"EPSG:4326",protocol,version));
    [Fact] public void GeoJsonProjectsFeaturesAndPreservesPolygonHole()
    {
        var layer = FeatureLayers.FromGeoJson(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory,"samples","sites.geojson")),"test");
        Assert.Equal(3,layer.Features.Count());
        var area = Assert.IsType<Polygon>(Assert.IsType<GeometryFeature>(layer.Features.Last()).Geometry);
        Assert.Equal(1,area.NumInteriorRings); Assert.Equal(3857,area.SRID); Assert.True(area.EnvelopeInternal.MinX > 1000000);
    }
    [Fact] public void GeometryStylesDrawLinesAndPolygonsRatherThanVertices()
    {
        var style=(Mapsui.Styles.Thematics.ThemeStyle)FeatureLayers.GeometryStyle(false);
        var features=FeatureLayers.Graphics().Features.ToArray();
        Assert.IsType<Mapsui.Styles.SymbolStyle>(style.GetStyle(features[0],new Viewport()));
        Assert.IsType<Mapsui.Styles.VectorStyle>(style.GetStyle(features[1],new Viewport()));
        Assert.IsType<Mapsui.Styles.VectorStyle>(style.GetStyle(features[2],new Viewport()));
    }
    [Fact] public void HighlightClonesAndClearsWithoutChangingOriginal()
    {
        var features = FeatureLayers.Graphics(); var item = (GeometryFeature)features.Features.First();
        using var highlight = FeatureLayers.Highlight(); FeatureLayers.Select(highlight,item);
        Assert.Single(highlight.Features); Assert.NotSame(item,highlight.Features.First());
        Assert.NotSame(item.Geometry,((GeometryFeature)highlight.Features.First()).Geometry);
        FeatureLayers.Select(highlight,null); Assert.Empty(highlight.Features); Assert.Equal(3,features.Features.Count());
    }
    [Fact] public void LayerOrderingIsBottomToTop()
    {
        using var map = new Map(); var a=FeatureLayers.Graphics(); var b=FeatureLayers.Highlight();
        map.Layers.Add(a); map.Layers.Add(b); map.Layers.MoveToTop(a); Assert.Same(a,map.Layers.Last());
        map.Layers.Remove(a); Assert.Single(map.Layers);
    }
    [Fact] public void OldRequestCannotCommitAfterNewerRequestOrDisposal()
    {
        using var latest = new LatestRequest(); var first=latest.Begin(); var second=latest.Begin();
        Assert.True(first.Token.IsCancellationRequested); Assert.False(latest.IsCurrent(first.Generation)); Assert.True(latest.IsCurrent(second.Generation));
        latest.Dispose(); Assert.False(latest.IsCurrent(second.Generation));
    }
    [Fact] public void FileLogExcludesExceptionPayload()
    {
        var folder=Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(),"ogc-log-test-"+Guid.NewGuid());
        try
        {
            using var provider=new FileLoggerProvider(folder); var logger=provider.CreateLogger("test");
            logger.LogWarning(new Exception("PASSWORD-secret-url"),"Transport failed");
            var text=File.ReadAllText(Directory.GetFiles(folder).Single()); Assert.Contains("Transport failed",text); Assert.DoesNotContain("PASSWORD",text);
        }
        finally { Directory.Delete(folder,true); }
    }
    [Fact] public void InvalidConfigurationFailsBeforeNetworking()
    {
        Assert.Throws<DemoException>(() => new ServiceOptions {Url="http://internal/wms"}.Validate());
        Assert.Throws<DemoException>(() => new ServiceOptions {Crs="EPSG:2039"}.Validate());
        Assert.Throws<DemoException>(() => new ServiceOptions {Kind="WMTS",Version="1.0.0"}.Validate());
    }
    [Fact] public void ExcessFeaturesAndInheritedWrongCrsAreRejected()
    {
        var xml=OgcFixture.Collection("<gml:Point><gml:pos>10 20</gml:pos></gml:Point>");
        Assert.Throws<DemoException>(()=>GmlFeatures.Read(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(xml),0));
        xml=xml.Replace("<wfs:FeatureCollection ","<wfs:FeatureCollection srsName=\"EPSG:4326\" ");
        Assert.Throws<DemoException>(()=>GmlFeatures.Read(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(xml),10));
    }
    [Fact] public void XmlExternalEntitiesAreRejected()
    {
        var xml="<!DOCTYPE x [<!ENTITY e SYSTEM \"file:///missing\">]><x>&e;</x>";
        Assert.Throws<DemoException>(()=>OgcTransport.ParseXml(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(xml)));
    }
    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Geometries()
    {
        var p="<gml:Point><gml:pos>10 20</gml:pos></gml:Point>";
        var l="<gml:LineString><gml:posList>0 0 10 10</gml:posList></gml:LineString>";
        var a="<gml:Polygon><gml:exterior><gml:LinearRing><gml:posList>0 0 10 0 10 10 0 0</gml:posList></gml:LinearRing></gml:exterior></gml:Polygon>";
        yield return [p,"Point"]; yield return [l,"LineString"]; yield return [a,"Polygon"];
        yield return [$"<gml:MultiPoint><gml:pointMember>{p}</gml:pointMember></gml:MultiPoint>","MultiPoint"];
        yield return [$"<gml:MultiCurve><gml:curveMember>{l}</gml:curveMember></gml:MultiCurve>","MultiLineString"];
        yield return [$"<gml:MultiSurface><gml:surfaceMember>{a}</gml:surfaceMember></gml:MultiSurface>","MultiPolygon"];
    }
    [Theory, MemberData(nameof(Geometries))]
    public void GmlGeometrySubset(string xml,string type)
    {
        foreach(var version in new[]{"1.1.0","2.0.0"})
        {
            var features=GmlFeatures.Read(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(OgcFixture.Collection(xml,version)),10);
            Assert.Single(features); Assert.Equal(type,features[0].Geometry!.GeometryType); Assert.Equal("Fixture site",features[0]["name"]);
        }
    }
    [Theory]
    [InlineData("<gml:Point srsName=\"EPSG:4326\"><gml:pos>10 20</gml:pos></gml:Point>")]
    [InlineData("<gml:Point srsDimension=\"3\"><gml:pos>10 20 30</gml:pos></gml:Point>")]
    [InlineData("<gml:Curve><gml:posList>0 0 10 10</gml:posList></gml:Curve>")]
    public void UnsupportedGmlFailsClearly(string xml) => Assert.Throws<DemoException>(()=>GmlFeatures.Read(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(OgcFixture.Collection(xml)),10));
}
``````

<a id="file-tests-ogcdemo-tests-ogcfixture-cs"></a>

### `tests/OgcDemo.Tests/OgcFixture.cs`

Source SHA-256: `cf6f079ffb2dfdfb30c575dd69e7fa29ef119dd3a243769643e5bb0ebe432d97`

``````csharp
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Text;

namespace OgcDemo.Tests;

/// <summary>Real loopback HTTP, generated OGC documents, no work endpoints or data.</summary>
public sealed class OgcFixture : IDisposable
{
    private readonly HttpListener _listener = new();
    private readonly CancellationTokenSource _stop = new();
    public ConcurrentQueue<(string Request, string? Authorization, string Query)> Requests { get; } = new();
    public string Url { get; }
    public bool RequireAuth { get; set; }
    public string Crs { get; set; } = "EPSG:3857";
    public string Geometry { get; set; } = "<gml:Point srsName=\"EPSG:3857\"><gml:pos>100 200</gml:pos></gml:Point>";
    public string? Failure { get; set; }
    public string ExtraWmtsResources { get; set; } = "";
    public static readonly byte[] Png = Convert.FromBase64String("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR4nGOQzFvwHwAD8gInMMAcvAAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==");
    public OgcFixture()
    {
        using var socket = new TcpListener(IPAddress.Loopback, 0); socket.Start();
        var port = ((IPEndPoint)socket.LocalEndpoint).Port; socket.Stop();
        Url = $"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/ogc";
        _listener.Prefixes.Add($"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/"); _listener.Start(); _ = ServeAsync();
    }
    private async Task ServeAsync()
    {
        while (!_stop.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            HttpListenerContext context;
            try { context = await _listener.GetContextAsync(); } catch { break; }
            _ = RespondAsync(context);
        }
    }
    private async Task RespondAsync(HttpListenerContext c)
    {
        try
        {
            var q = c.Request.QueryString;
            var request = q["request"] ?? "GetTile";
            var version = q["version"] ?? "1.3.0";
            Requests.Enqueue((request, c.Request.Headers["Authorization"], c.Request.Url!.Query));
            if (RequireAuth && c.Request.Headers["Authorization"] != "Basic ZGVtbzpwYXNz") { c.Response.StatusCode = 401; return; }
            if (Failure == "timeout") { await Task.Delay(2500, _stop.Token); return; }
            if (Failure == "redirect") { c.Response.StatusCode=302; c.Response.RedirectLocation="https://different.example.invalid/"; return; }
            if (Failure == "slowbody") { c.Response.SendChunked=true; await c.Response.OutputStream.WriteAsync(new byte[]{120}); await c.Response.OutputStream.FlushAsync(); await Task.Delay(2500,_stop.Token); return; }
            if (Failure == "http") { c.Response.StatusCode = 503; return; }
            string? text = Failure switch
            {
                "xml" => "<broken>",
                "exception" => "<ServiceExceptionReport><ServiceException>secret server detail</ServiceException></ServiceExceptionReport>",
                "large" or "stream" => new string('x', 4096),
                _ => request switch
                {
                    "GetCapabilities" when q["service"] == "WMTS" => Wmts(),
                    "GetCapabilities" when q["service"] == "WFS" => Wfs(version),
                    "GetCapabilities" => Wms(version),
                    "DescribeFeatureType" => "<xs:schema xmlns:xs=\"http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema\" targetNamespace=\"urn:demo\"><xs:element name=\"sites\" type=\"xs:anyType\"/></xs:schema>",
                    "GetFeature" => Collection(Geometry, version),
                    "GetFeatureInfo" => "name: synthetic WMS result",
                    _ => null
                }
            };
            var bytes = text is null ? Png : Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(text);
            c.Response.ContentType = text is null ? "image/png" : request == "GetFeatureInfo" || Failure is "large" or "stream" ? "text/plain" : "text/xml";
            if (Failure=="stream") c.Response.SendChunked=true; else c.Response.ContentLength64 = bytes.Length; await c.Response.OutputStream.WriteAsync(bytes);
        }
        catch (Exception) when (_stop.IsCancellationRequested) { }
        catch (HttpListenerException) { }
        finally { c.Response.Close(); }
    }
    public string Wms(string version) => $$"""
        <{{(version == "1.3.0" ? "WMS_Capabilities" : "WMT_MS_Capabilities")}} version="{{version}}" {{(version == "1.3.0" ? "xmlns=\"http://www.opengis.net/wms\"" : "")}} xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink">
          <Service><Name>WMS</Name><Title>Fixture</Title><OnlineResource xlink:href="{{Url}}"/></Service>
          <Capability><Request>
            <GetCapabilities><Format>text/xml</Format><DCPType><HTTP><Get><OnlineResource xlink:href="{{Url}}"/></Get></HTTP></DCPType></GetCapabilities>
            <GetMap><Format>image/png</Format><DCPType><HTTP><Get><OnlineResource xlink:href="{{Url}}"/></Get></HTTP></DCPType></GetMap>
            <GetFeatureInfo><Format>text/plain</Format><DCPType><HTTP><Get><OnlineResource xlink:href="{{Url}}"/></Get></HTTP></DCPType></GetFeatureInfo>
          </Request><Exception><Format>XML</Format></Exception>
          <Layer queryable="1"><Title>Fixture layers</Title><{{(version == "1.3.0" ? "CRS" : "SRS")}}>{{Crs}}</{{(version == "1.3.0" ? "CRS" : "SRS")}}>
          <Layer><Name>demo:sites</Name><Title>Sites</Title><BoundingBox {{(version == "1.3.0" ? "CRS" : "SRS")}}="{{Crs}}" minx="0" miny="0" maxx="1000" maxy="1000"/></Layer></Layer>
          </Capability>
        </{{(version == "1.3.0" ? "WMS_Capabilities" : "WMT_MS_Capabilities")}}>
        """;
    public string Wfs(string version) => $$"""
        <wfs:WFS_Capabilities xmlns:wfs="http://www.opengis.net/wfs{{(version == "2.0.0" ? "/2.0" : "")}}" xmlns:ows="http://www.opengis.net/ows/1.1" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" xmlns:demo="urn:demo" version="{{version}}">
        <ows:OperationsMetadata>
          <ows:Operation name="DescribeFeatureType"><ows:DCP><ows:HTTP><ows:Get xlink:href="{{Url}}"/></ows:HTTP></ows:DCP></ows:Operation>
          <ows:Operation name="GetFeature"><ows:DCP><ows:HTTP><ows:Get xlink:href="{{Url}}"/></ows:HTTP></ows:DCP>
          <ows:Parameter name="outputFormat"><ows:AllowedValues><ows:Value>{{(version == "2.0.0" ? "application/gml+xml; version=3.2" : "text/xml; subtype=gml/3.1.1")}}</ows:Value></ows:AllowedValues></ows:Parameter></ows:Operation>
        </ows:OperationsMetadata>
        <wfs:FeatureTypeList><wfs:FeatureType><wfs:Name>demo:sites</wfs:Name><wfs:{{(version == "2.0.0" ? "DefaultCRS" : "DefaultSRS")}}>{{Crs}}</wfs:{{(version == "2.0.0" ? "DefaultCRS" : "DefaultSRS")}}></wfs:FeatureType></wfs:FeatureTypeList>
        </wfs:WFS_Capabilities>
        """;
    public string Wmts() => $$"""
        <Capabilities xmlns="http://www.opengis.net/wmts/1.0" xmlns:ows="http://www.opengis.net/ows/1.1" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" version="1.0.0">
          <ows:ServiceIdentification><ows:Title>Fixture</ows:Title><ows:ServiceType>OGC WMTS</ows:ServiceType><ows:ServiceTypeVersion>1.0.0</ows:ServiceTypeVersion></ows:ServiceIdentification>
          <Contents><Layer><ows:Title>Fixture tiles</ows:Title><ows:Identifier>demo:sites</ows:Identifier>
            <Style isDefault="true"><ows:Identifier>default</ows:Identifier></Style><Format>image/png</Format>
            <TileMatrixSetLink><TileMatrixSet>WebMercatorQuad</TileMatrixSet></TileMatrixSetLink>
            <ResourceURL format="image/png" resourceType="tile" template="{{Url}}?request=GetTile&amp;TileMatrix={TileMatrix}&amp;TileRow={TileRow}&amp;TileCol={TileCol}"/>
            {{ExtraWmtsResources}}
          </Layer><TileMatrixSet><ows:Identifier>WebMercatorQuad</ows:Identifier><ows:SupportedCRS>{{Crs}}</ows:SupportedCRS>
            <TileMatrix><ows:Identifier>0</ows:Identifier><ScaleDenominator>559082264.0287178</ScaleDenominator><TopLeftCorner>-20037508.3427892 20037508.3427892</TopLeftCorner><TileWidth>256</TileWidth><TileHeight>256</TileHeight><MatrixWidth>1</MatrixWidth><MatrixHeight>1</MatrixHeight></TileMatrix>
          </TileMatrixSet></Contents>
        </Capabilities>
        """;
    public static string Collection(string geometry, string version = "2.0.0") => $$"""
        <wfs:FeatureCollection xmlns:wfs="http://www.opengis.net/wfs{{(version == "2.0.0" ? "/2.0" : "")}}" xmlns:gml="http://www.opengis.net/gml{{(version == "2.0.0" ? "/3.2" : "")}}" xmlns:demo="urn:demo"><{{(version == "2.0.0" ? "wfs:member" : "gml:featureMember")}}><demo:sites gml:id="site.1"><demo:name>Fixture site</demo:name><demo:geometry>{{geometry}}</demo:geometry></demo:sites></{{(version == "2.0.0" ? "wfs:member" : "gml:featureMember")}}></wfs:FeatureCollection>
        """;
    public void Dispose() { _stop.Cancel(); _listener.Close(); _stop.Dispose(); }
}
``````

<a id="file-tests-ogcdemo-tests-protocoltests-cs"></a>

### `tests/OgcDemo.Tests/ProtocolTests.cs`

Source SHA-256: `92a468c6723ac173767cdb509d8a77a3b85940849d08fdfca5f1100da7dbcebd`

``````csharp
using BruTile;
using Mapsui;
using Mapsui.Layers;
using Mapsui.Tiling.Layers;
using OgcDemo.Core;
using Xunit;

namespace OgcDemo.Tests;

public class ProtocolTests
{
    private static ServiceOptions Options(OgcFixture f,string kind,string version) => new()
    { Url=f.Url,Kind=kind,Version=version,Layer="demo:sites",Style=kind=="WMTS"?"default":"", MatrixSet="WebMercatorQuad",MaxFeatures=10 };
    private static OgcTransport Transport(ServiceOptions o) => new(o,allowLoopbackHttp:true);
    [Theory] [InlineData("1.3.0")] [InlineData("1.1.1")]
    public async Task WmsCapabilitiesMapAndIdentify(string version)
    {
        using var f=new OgcFixture(); var o=Options(f,"WMS",version);
        using var t=Transport(o); using var layer=await OgcLayers.ConnectAsync(o,t,default);
        Assert.True(layer.Queryable);
        var url=layer.Wms!.GetRequestUrl(new MRect(0,0,1000,1000),256,256);
        Assert.Equal(OgcFixture.Png, await t.GetAsync(url));
        var text=await OgcLayers.IdentifyAsync(layer,new MRect(0,0,1000,1000),256,256,10,20,default);
        Assert.Contains("synthetic WMS",text);
        Assert.Contains(f.Requests,r=>r.Request=="GetFeatureInfo" && r.Query.Contains(version=="1.3.0"?"i=10":"x=10"));
    }
    [Theory] [InlineData("1.1.0")] [InlineData("2.0.0")]
    public async Task WfsCapabilitiesSchemaAndBoundedFeatures(string version)
    {
        using var f=new OgcFixture(); var o=Options(f,"WFS",version);
        using var t=Transport(o); var client=new WfsClient(o,t); await client.InitializeAsync(default);
        var features=await client.FetchAsync(new MRect(0,0,1000,1000),default); Assert.Single(features);
        Assert.Contains(f.Requests,r=>r.Request=="DescribeFeatureType");
        var featureRequest=f.Requests.Single(r=>r.Request=="GetFeature");
        Assert.Contains(version=="2.0.0"?"count=10":"maxFeatures=10",featureRequest.Query); Assert.Contains("bbox=",featureRequest.Query);
    }
    [Fact] public async Task WmtsUsesExplicitMatrixAndReturnsTile()
    {
        using var f=new OgcFixture(); var o=Options(f,"WMTS","1.0.0"); using var t=Transport(o);
        using var layer=await OgcLayers.ConnectAsync(o,t,default);
        var tiles=(TileLayer)layer.Layer;
        using var http=new HttpClient();
        var bytes=await ((BruTile.IHttpTileSource)tiles.TileSource).GetTileAsync(http,new TileInfo {Index=new TileIndex(0,0,0)});
        Assert.Equal(OgcFixture.Png,bytes);
        o.MatrixSet="not-advertised"; await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DemoException>(()=>OgcLayers.ConnectAsync(o,t,default));
    }
    [Fact] public async Task WmtsNeverRequestsTileJsonOrFeatureInfoAsAnImage()
    {
        using var f = new OgcFixture();
        f.ExtraWmtsResources = $"<ResourceURL format=\"application/json\" resourceType=\"TileJSON\" template=\"{f.Url}?request=GetTileJSON\"/>" +
            $"<ResourceURL format=\"text/plain\" resourceType=\"FeatureInfo\" template=\"{f.Url}?request=GetFeatureInfo\"/>" +
            $"<ResourceURL format=\"image/jpeg\" resourceType=\"tile\" template=\"{f.Url}?request=WrongFormat\"/>";
        var o = Options(f, "WMTS", "1.0.0"); using var transport = Transport(o);
        using var connection = await OgcLayers.ConnectAsync(o, transport, default);
        var source = (BruTile.IHttpTileSource)((TileLayer)connection.Layer).TileSource;
        using var http = new HttpClient();
        for (var i = 0; i < 32; i++) Assert.Equal(OgcFixture.Png, await source.GetTileAsync(http, new TileInfo { Index = new TileIndex(0, 0, 0) }));
        Assert.Equal(32, f.Requests.Count(r => r.Request == "GetTile"));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(f.Requests, r => r.Request is "GetTileJSON" or "GetFeatureInfo" or "WrongFormat");
    }
    [Theory] [InlineData("WMS","1.3.0")] [InlineData("WMTS","1.0.0")] [InlineData("WFS","2.0.0")]
    public async Task AuthenticationCoversDiscoveryAndSubsequentRequests(string kind,string version)
    {
        using var f=new OgcFixture {RequireAuth=true}; var o=Options(f,kind,version); o.Authentication="Basic";
        o.CredentialPrefix="OGC_TEST_"+Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
        Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(o.CredentialPrefix+"_USERNAME","demo"); Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(o.CredentialPrefix+"_PASSWORD","pass");
        try
        {
            using var t=Transport(o); using var layer=await OgcLayers.ConnectAsync(o,t,default);
            if(kind=="WFS") await layer.Wfs!.FetchAsync(new MRect(0,0,1000,1000),default);
            if(kind=="WMS") { await t.GetAsync(layer.Wms!.GetRequestUrl(new MRect(0,0,1000,1000),100,100)); await OgcLayers.IdentifyAsync(layer,new MRect(0,0,1000,1000),100,100,10,10,default); }
            if(kind=="WMTS") { using var http=new HttpClient(); await ((BruTile.IHttpTileSource)((TileLayer)layer.Layer).TileSource).GetTileAsync(http,new TileInfo {Index=new TileIndex(0,0,0)}); }
            Assert.True(f.Requests.Count>=2); Assert.All(f.Requests,r=>Assert.Equal("Basic ZGVtbzpwYXNz",r.Authorization));
        }
        finally { Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(o.CredentialPrefix+"_USERNAME",null); Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(o.CredentialPrefix+"_PASSWORD",null); }
    }
    [Theory] [InlineData("WMS","1.3.0")] [InlineData("WFS","2.0.0")] [InlineData("WMTS","1.0.0")]
    public async Task UnsupportedServiceCrsRejected(string kind,string version)
    {
        using var f=new OgcFixture {Crs="EPSG:4326"}; var o=Options(f,kind,version); using var t=Transport(o);
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DemoException>(()=>OgcLayers.ConnectAsync(o,t,default));
    }
    [Theory] [InlineData("xml")] [InlineData("exception")] [InlineData("http")] [InlineData("large")] [InlineData("timeout")] [InlineData("stream")] [InlineData("slowbody")] [InlineData("redirect")]
    public async Task FailureIsBoundedAndSanitized(string failure)
    {
        using var f=new OgcFixture {Failure=failure}; var o=Options(f,"WMS","1.3.0"); o.MaxResponseBytes=1024; o.TimeoutSeconds=1;
        using var t=Transport(o); var e=await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DemoException>(()=>t.GetAsync(f.Url)); Assert.DoesNotContain("secret",e.Message);
    }
    [Fact] public async Task UnauthorizedFailsAndCrossOriginIsNeverSent()
    {
        using var f=new OgcFixture {RequireAuth=true}; using var t=Transport(Options(f,"WMS","1.3.0"));
        Assert.Contains("401",(await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DemoException>(()=>t.GetAsync(f.Url))).Message);
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<DemoException>(()=>t.GetAsync("https://different.example.invalid/data")); Assert.Single(f.Requests);
    }
    [Fact] public async Task CallerCancellationStopsRequest()
    {
        using var f=new OgcFixture {Failure="timeout"}; using var t=Transport(Options(f,"WMS","1.3.0"));
        using var cts=new CancellationTokenSource(30); await Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync<OperationCanceledException>(()=>t.GetAsync(f.Url,cts.Token));
    }
}
``````

### `tools/ManualBuilder/Program.cs`

Source SHA-256: `cd87a8f372caafd9a8f14c5c182857032293775fe37dfdd4938d9b7573e7a36b`

``````csharp
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

var check = args.Contains("--check");
var root = args.FirstOrDefault(a => a != "--check") ?? Directory.GetCurrentDirectory();
root = Path.GetFullPath(root);
if (!File.Exists(Path.Combine(root, "OgcDemo.slnx"))) throw new Exception("Run from the repository root, or pass its path.");
var manifest = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Lesson[]>(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root,"docs/tutorial.json")),new JsonSerializerOptions {PropertyNameCaseInsensitive=true})!;
var outputs = new Dictionary<string,string>();
var included = new HashSet<string>();
string Anchor(string path) => "file-" + Regex.Replace(path.ToLowerInvariant(),"[^a-z0-9]","-");
string Source(string path, bool standalone)
{
    included.Add(path);
    var content=File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root,path)).Replace("\r\n","\n").TrimEnd();
    var lang=Path.GetExtension(path) switch {".cs"=>"csharp",".json"=>"json",".geojson"=>"json",".ps1"=>"powershell",".csproj" or ".props" or ".slnx" or ".Config" or ".xaml"=>"xml",_=>"text"};
    return $"\n<a id=\"{Anchor(path)}\"></a>\n\n### `{path}`\n\n" + (standalone ? "" : $"Source SHA-256: `{Convert.ToHexStringLower(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(content+"\n")))}`\n\n") +
        $"``````{lang}\n{content}\n``````\n";
}
string ManualLinks(string prose, string file) => Regex.Replace(prose,@"\]\(([^)]+)\)",m =>
{
    var target=m.Groups[1].Value;
    if(target.StartsWith('#') || Uri.TryCreate(target,UriKind.Absolute,out _)) return m.Value;
    var path=Path.GetRelativePath(root,Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(root,Path.GetDirectoryName(file)!,target.Split('#')[0]))).Replace('\\','/');
    if(path=="docs/BUILD-GUIDE.md") return "](#contents)";
    return "](#"+Anchor(path)+")";
});
var manual=new StringBuilder("# Build a .NET 10 WPF GIS Application with Mapsui and OGC Services\n\nGenerated from the reference project. English tutorial, complete source, tests, and configuration.\n\nDo not edit this file; edit docs/templates and source, then run ManualBuilder.\n\n## Contents\n\n");
foreach(var lesson in manifest) manual.AppendLine($"- [{lesson.Title}](#{Anchor(lesson.Output)})");
foreach(var lesson in manifest)
{
    var prose=File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root,lesson.Template)).Replace("\r\n","\n").TrimEnd()+"\n";
    var chapter=new StringBuilder("<!-- Generated by ManualBuilder. Edit the matching docs/templates file. -->\n\n"+prose);
    manual.Append($"\n<a id=\"{Anchor(lesson.Output)}\"></a>\n\n{ManualLinks(prose,lesson.Output)}");
    if(lesson.Sources.Length>0) { chapter.Append("\n## Complete source for this lesson\n"); manual.Append("\n## Complete source for this lesson\n"); }
    foreach(var path in lesson.Sources)
    {
        var alreadyIncluded=included.Contains(path); chapter.Append(Source(path,true));
        if(alreadyIncluded) manual.Append($"\nComplete source: [{path}](#{Anchor(path)}) (listed earlier, unchanged).\n");
        else manual.Append(Source(path,false));
    }
    outputs[lesson.Output]=chapter.ToString();
}
var extras = new[]{"README.md","LLM_CONTEXT.md","docs/configuration.md","docs/api-reference.md","docs/requirements.md","docs/validation.md","docs/extensions/advanced.md"};
foreach(var path in extras)
{
    manual.Append($"\n<a id=\"{Anchor(path)}\"></a>\n\n");
    manual.Append(ManualLinks(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root,path)).Replace("\r\n","\n"),path)); manual.AppendLine();
}
manual.Append("\n# Remaining project files\n\nTogether with the lesson listings, these reproduce the reference project. Generated bin/obj, private settings, and validation tooling are excluded.\n");
var sourceRoots=new[]{"src","tests","tools","scripts","samples"};
var files=sourceRoots.SelectMany(d=>Directory.EnumerateFiles(Path.Combine(root,d),"*",SearchOption.AllDirectories))
    .Where(p=>!p.Replace('\\','/').Split('/').Any(s=>s is "bin" or "obj"))
    .Select(p=>Path.GetRelativePath(root,p).Replace('\\','/'))
    .Concat(manifest.Select(l=>l.Template))
    .Concat(extras)
    .Concat(new[]{".gitignore","global.json","Directory.Build.props","NuGet.Config","OgcDemo.slnx","docs/tutorial.json"})
    .Where(p=>!p.EndsWith("appsettings.local.json") && !included.Contains(p)).OrderBy(p=>p,StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray();
foreach(var path in files) { var section=Source(path,false); if(extras.Contains(path)) section=section.Replace(Anchor(path),Anchor(path)+"-raw"); manual.Append(section); }
outputs["docs/BUILD-GUIDE.md"]=manual.ToString();
var guide=outputs["docs/BUILD-GUIDE.md"];
var withoutCode=Regex.Replace(guide,"(?ms)^``````.*?^``````[^\\n]*","");
var ids=Regex.Matches(withoutCode,"<a id=\"([^\"]+)\">").Select(m=>m.Groups[1].Value).ToHashSet();
ids.Add("contents");
foreach(Match m in Regex.Matches(withoutCode,@"\]\(#([^)]+)\)"))
    if(!ids.Contains(m.Groups[1].Value)) throw new Exception("Missing manual anchor: "+m.Groups[1].Value);
int changed=0;
foreach(var (path, rawContent) in outputs)
{
    var content=rawContent.Replace("\r\n","\n");
    var full=Path.Combine(root,path);
    if(File.Exists(full) && File.ReadAllText(full).Replace("\r\n","\n")==content) continue;
    changed++; if(check) Console.WriteLine("Stale or missing: "+path);
    else { Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(full)!); File.WriteAllText(full,content,new UTF8Encoding(false)); }
}
// Validate local links in authored documentation. Code fences are removed first.
foreach(var file in extras.Concat(manifest.Select(l=>l.Template)))
{
    var text=File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(root,file));
    text=Regex.Replace(text,"(?ms)^```.*?^```[^\\n]*","");
    foreach(Match m in Regex.Matches(text,@"\]\(([^)]+)\)"))
    {
        var target=m.Groups[1].Value.Split('#')[0];
        if(target.Length==0 || Uri.TryCreate(target,UriKind.Absolute,out _)) continue;
        // Templates use links relative to their generated chapter directory (same depth).
        if(!File.Exists(Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(root,Path.GetDirectoryName(file)!,target))) && !Directory.Exists(Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(root,Path.GetDirectoryName(file)!,target))))
            throw new Exception($"Broken link in {file}: {target}");
    }
}
Console.WriteLine(check ? $"Documentation check: {changed} stale outputs; local links valid." : $"Generated {outputs.Count} documents; {included.Count} source files included; local links valid.");
return check && changed>0 ? 1 : 0;
record Lesson(string Title,string Template,string Output,string[] Sources);
``````
